# A013 / B03 representation stability

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Transport every finite ordered gate union through actual physical restriction, disjoint sign tensor product, and physical multiplexer composition, explicitly accounting for gates, regular inner functions/orders, labels, reference-root probabilities and normalizers. Entropy and complete reference costs are in bits; influence is total physical flip probability. Fresh independent selectors and disjoint tensor blocks are hypotheses, not inferred after reuse.

New dependency-consuming result: for every balanced Boolean core g with complete nonempty subprobability reference cost c_g, r>=1 fresh point controls, p=2^-r, F=1+A_r(g-1), an explicit full normalized reference has cross entropy

    C_F <= h2(2p-p^2)+2r(2p-p^2)+p*c_g
        <= p*c_g+(6+2/ln2)*p*r.

If c_g<=K I(g), its full cost is <=max(K,6+2/ln2)I(F). The constant is below 8.886 and does not grow with disjoint tensor/reference composition, fresh-character balancing, or balanced-core activation depth. The arbitrary core cost remains present. This closes the activated-literal transcript failure at the complete projected-reference level, not at the original incidence-queue infimum. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

COVER_STABILITY.tex is the complete standalone proof; STABILITY_LEDGER.json is the operation-by-operation interface. Restriction commutes with union and actual inner-gate restriction, preserves inherited desirability orders, removes relevant coordinates, and makes p_l conditional on the fixed physical event. Rank decreases but rank divided by occupied predecessor count need not decrease. Root odds and normalizers must therefore be recomputed; no unproved monotonicity is used. Independent root-vector entropy is sum h2(q_l), and weighted label divergence has the explicit bound log2(2^|A| W/W_a), when W_a>0.

Tensor cover uses the supplied covers of both signs, with positive gate count half the sum of the products of signed count sums and differences. Tensor probabilities multiply only on disjoint physical blocks. A negative cover can be formed as one intersection of all complements but its regular-inner count can worsen. The tensor minterm cover of t independent literals has 2^(t-1) positive gates despite zero spectral entropy. This separates cover growth from paid complete reference cost.

Fresh multiplexers attach the actual selector cell to each branch gate and add its path literals. Selector overlap imposes a new incidence weight scale. Reused-selector multiplexers first restrict the branch to the selected physical assignment; g_+(x)=x,g_-(x)=-x on selector x computes the constant +1. Root probabilities based on unreduced formal branches are invalid.

For activation, sum the old transcript's Kraft masses over all witnesses decoding the same Fourier set. Constant stopping leaves contribute Q(S,empty)=sum_(j>=max S)4^-j, while core leaves contribute Q(S,T)=p^2 R_g(T). These are actual projected reference masses, not an asserted incoherent Fourier decomposition. Their total mass is <=1. The exact Boolean spectrum has empty atom (1-p)^2, control-only nonempty atoms p^2, and core atoms p^2 ghat(T)^2. Replace the projected empty atom by its actual mass and normalize the entire nonempty reference. Exact cross entropy retains the correction v log2(q_star), with its nonpositive sign. Bounding the remaining terms proves the complete cost above; no leaf-label or terminal cost is discarded without a valid reference. Products of full references add cost; character translation balances without changing cost; activation weights all older cost and influence by the same p. Expanding recurrences pays each local activation charge once.

First unsupported universal line: every arbitrary balanced Boolean core admits a complete reference with c_g<=K I(g) for one absolute K. It is not assumed. Nor is the original queue's variational infimum bounded by this alternative reference. Arbitrary physical data restrictions can make the core unbalanced, and restriction may destroy a retained tree's boundary or orthogonality admission; pruning/re-certification is separate work.


Late lead-directed supplement: COVER_STABILITY.tex final two sections prove that all three Section 5 helper-charge expectations may strictly increase on one actual restriction of a read-two union. The core is h=(z AND y1) OR (z AND y2), with mean -1/4, I=5/4. Activate on r=10 point controls and restrict those controls active. Parent I=55/4096. Parent label/subset/rank-rarity upper bounds are 5/1024,55/1024,231/1024; child values/lower bounds are 17/16,1/16,3/10. Rank/rarity here means the actual G_j helper before averaging; the coarse (24/5)I log2 k term also rises from 33/512 to 6. These statements hold irrespective of assignment optimization. The rare child's physical probability 1/1024 is retained, so this refutes pointwise monotonicity, not expected stopping-tree transport.

The lead's four-level shared-selector request is fulfilled by F_0=y,F_d=MUX_x(F_(d-1),z_d), d=1..4. Formal read d+1 reduces to actual read two, with two gates of probability 1/4, lambda=1/2, root odds 1/8, root probability 1/9, Lambda=2 log2(9/8), root-vector entropy 2 h2(1/9), attachment selector-name entropy one bit, H=2,I=3/2. Contradictory intermediate clauses have zero probability and are removed. Exact four-depth tables and root ledger are in out/LEAD_LEDGER_RESULTS.json.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md supplies the complete all-parameter proof. Exact conserved ownership B=2(d*-delta)+B_C prevents reusing collision influence. The three-bit rare regime gives B/d*<29/8 when d*<=1/32. The dimension-free moderate regime gives B<=2d*+log2(5/4)<4d* for d*>=1/6. Their full-reference surcharge is K=4+2/ln2<7, with exactly half the calibrated terminal KL debts and exact full label cost.

Actual one-point edits have delta=8d^2 and an explicit B formula. All 2,048 labelled phase/point pairs fit four proved signed Walsh multisets, by integer divisibility, Parseval and Fourier inversion. Eight outward rational certificates close r=0,1; the rare proof closes all r>=2. Thus the result is infinite in controller width, not a finite search extrapolation. # A014 — interference congestion with a paid exceptional mass

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Run: `20261011T223346Z-fleet05-A014-overlap-beyond-worst-case-read`, B03, A014. Full FEI remains OPEN. This stage proves the general interference lemma requested in B03 lead notes v1, supplies a complete queue-cost criterion, checks PP2 and PP3 exactly, and proves an L2 repair paying the PP_q rank/rarity bill for every prime q. Independent review is pending. The complete unbounded-read calibration is addressing, an already established FEI class; no new complete class theorem or priority claim is made.

All cubes are uniform; outputs are +/-1; Fourier coefficients are probability-normalized; entropy and all displayed costs use bits. Let f be an OR of N nonconstant Boolean gates h_l, each a combination of at most M regular functions. Let R_l be its relevant support, p_l=P(h_l=1), pi_l=min(p_l,1/2), b_l=log2(1/pi_l), a=6/5, gamma=120/11. Remove false constant gates; a true constant gate makes f constant and immediate. Set p(A)=fhat(A)^2, v=Var(f), I=sum_A |A|p(A).

For j with alpha_j=Inf_j(f)>0, partition d_j f into disjoint signed {0,+/-1} pieces d_jl by the least gate containing j that is pivotal when f is pivotal. Set alpha_jl=||d_jl||_2^2 and alpha_j=sum_l alpha_jl. Omit zero pieces. Define

    nu_j(T)=dhat_j(T)^2/alpha_j,
    rho_j(T)=sum_l dhat_jl(T)^2/alpha_j.

Both are probability laws. For A=T union {j}, let

    F_lj(A)=min_r ln[r_lr(j)/(|A intersect U_jlr|+1)],
    G_jl(A)=(a/ln 2) F_lj(A)+b_l 1[|A intersect R_l|<b_l/gamma],
    G_j(A)=min_{l:j in R_l} G_jl(A).

These are nonnegative. Let Gmax_j=max_T G_j(T union {j}). For any fixed kappa_j>=1 define the dimensionless exceptional mass

    epsilon_j(kappa_j)=sum_T (nu_j(T)-kappa_j rho_j(T))_+.

Zero denominators contribute zero: rho(T)=0 implies every piece amplitude and their sum vanish. No H(f) or assumed FEI inequality enters these definitions.

**Interference lemma.** For every such union and all choices kappa_j>=1,

    E_nu_j G_j <= E0+(24/5)log2 M+(24/5)log2 kappa_j
                  +epsilon_j(kappa_j) Gmax_j,                       (1)

where E0=(24/5)[log2(sqrt(2e)+2^(3/5))+log2 e]. The worst-event value is max_T nu_j(T)/rho_j(T), in which case epsilon_j=0. A smaller kappa is legal only with its explicit exceptional payment.

**Complete criterion.** Choose a global 0<lambda<=1 and the finite forest witness described below, using an assignment minimizing G_j. Write F(A) for the visited gates, s_l=|A_l|, ell_l=|A intersect R_l|. Let roots(A) be its roots and J_e the attaching coordinate of child edge e. Set

    Lambda_lambda=sum_l log2(1+lambda pi_l),
    S(A)=a sum_{l in F(A)} s_l log2(ell_l/s_l),
    L_lambda(A)=|roots(A)|log2(1/lambda)+sum_edges e log2 k_{J_e},

where k_i counts gates relevant to i. The following fully specified spectral congestion is sufficient:

    Gamma = [h2(v)+v Lambda_lambda+sum_{A!=empty}p(A)(S(A)+L_lambda(A))
              +(24/5)sum_j alpha_j log2 kappa_j
              +sum_j alpha_j epsilon_j(kappa_j)Gmax_j]/I.          (2)

For nonconstant f,

    H(f) <= [K0+E0+(29/5)log2 M+Gamma] I(f),                     (3)
    K0=120/11+3+2log2 3+log2(6e).

In particular the conservative bound K0+E0<218/5 is sufficient. This replaces all three worst-read costs by actual normalized root/label, subset, and interference bills. It does not assert a bound on Gamma for arbitrary unions. Gate/root names, normalization, and rarity are retained.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Execution protocol `fei-execution-protocol/2`, revision `2026-10-11-execution-v2`, read in full. Read README, CURRENT_STATUS, claim and obstruction registries, packet statuses, stream next actions and the plan's A014 contract and relevant joint-cost discussion. Large concatenated initial reads were truncated; source use is confined to the separately displayed relevant passages, not an asserted exhaustive audit of every line of the plan. Read campaign CAMPAIGN/CLAIMS/COUNTEREXAMPLES/NATIVE_MAPPING, the effective-piece seed lemma (SEED_LEMMAS section 1), and curated overlapping-product/log-allocation notes. Reviewed the three earlier B05/B09/B19 run reports for source scope; their theorems are not premises for (1)-(3).

Read BOARD snapshot 2026-10-11 22:26 UTC and B03 STREAM_NOTES v1, 22:30 UTC. The lead directs A014 to interference and PP2/PP3, leaving the full PP family analysis to the lead. **Specific disagreement:** least-pivotal-gate PP pieces are unequal, and the proposed worst-event kappa is unbounded, even though their masses become asymptotically equal. No peer file was modified.

Main mathematical source: `context/threshold_paper_38pp.txt`, Section 5, Theorem 5.1, Lemma 5.3, equations (30)-(37), and its source-exact copy `research_pushes/2026-10-11-full-fei-100-agents/baseline/current-threshold-paper.tex`. The text extraction obscures square-root signs; the TeX establishes B_*=sqrt(2e), not 2e, in the rank tail. Our conservative rounding also remains valid with the larger 2e. Read the earlier 29-page threshold introduction and both percolation introductions/theorem statements for scope; no percolation theorem is used. Source hashes and actual file lengths are recorded in `out/receipts.json`.

The proof uses the manuscript's signed-antichain rank tail and fixed-noise rarity tail, not the mass-only K_eff refinement. The refinement remains a valid baseline but gives no constant when equal piece masses spread over many pieces.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Proof of the interference lemma

For a piece d_jl, Section 5.4 gives the rank tail

    nu_jl(F_lj>t) <= M sqrt(2e) exp(-t/2),

and Lemma 5.3 gives

    nu_jl(ell_l<b_l/gamma) <= 2^(3/5) 2^(-5b_l/12).

If G_j>u then G_jl>u. Splitting its two nonnegative summands at u/2, with a=6/5, yields

    nu_jl(G_j>u) <= M (sqrt(2e)+2^(3/5)) 2^(-5u/24).             (4)

For the rarity event to contribute, b_l>u/2, so its probability has precisely the same exponent as the rank term. Weighted averaging with weights alpha_jl/alpha_j gives (4) for rho_j, with no read factor. These weights sum to one because physical supports are disjoint. Piecewise orthogonality alone without the source tail hypotheses would not establish (4).

Let delta_j(T)=(nu_j(T)-kappa_j rho_j(T))_+. Then for every E,

    nu_j(E) <= kappa_j rho_j(E)+delta_j(E).

Layer-cake integration over 0<=u<=Gmax_j gives

    E_nu_j G_j <= integral_0^infty min{1,kappa_j M A0 2^(-beta u)}du
                  +sum_T delta_j(T)G_j(T),

where A0=sqrt(2e)+2^(3/5), beta=5/24. To justify the minimum, nu(E)<=1 and nu(E)<=kappa rho(E)+delta(E) imply nu(E)<=min(1,kappa rho(E))+delta(E). The integral equals beta^(-1)[log2(kappa M A0)+log2 e], since kappa M A0>=1. Bound the last sum by epsilon_j Gmax_j. This proves (1). The sharper sum delta_j(T)G_j(T) can replace epsilon_j Gmax_j throughout.

A spectral-block sufficient condition is also useful. Suppose the fine pieces can be partitioned into groups of at most b pieces, and the sums D_B=sum_{l in B} d_jl have pairwise disjoint Fourier supports. At each T at most one grouped sum is nonzero, so

    dhat_j(T)^2=|Dhat_B(T)|^2 <= b sum_{l in B} dhat_jl(T)^2
                                          <= b sum_l dhat_jl(T)^2.

Thus kappa_j=b, epsilon_j=0 is valid. Fine-piece spectra need not be disjoint; cancellation inside a group is allowed. This criterion is different from merely assuming orthogonality in L2.

### 2. Proof of the complete queue criterion

Use the manuscript queue law, replacing the global root odds pi_l/k by lambda pi_l; retain subset selection, sizes, child counts and multisets. At attaching coordinate i choose uniformly among its k_i gates. The law remains a subprobability, since only successful finite runs contribute.

For each nonzero A, choose tau(j) minimizing G_jl(A), allowing a fixed deterministic tie rule depending on A; A_l={j:tau(j)=l}. Choose the manuscript directed spanning forest. Source components furnish roots. Distinct root supports intersect A in disjoint subsets, by the source-component argument in Section 5.3. The prescribed queue run visits each gate exactly once and outputs A.

Its root bill is Lambda_lambda+sum_roots[b_r+log2(1/lambda)]. Dense-root b_r's sum to at most gamma |A|, because b_r<=gamma ell_r and root intersections are disjoint. Each nondense root owns a variable, and its b_r is paid from the selected variable's G_j. All subset rank terms plus these sparse-root bills are therefore bounded by sum_{j in A}G_j(A), plus the dense-root charge gamma|A|.

The elementary symmetric normalizer obeys Z_s^l<=(6eM)^s. For an assigned variable i, its predecessor count plus one is <=ell_l, whence the subset weight bill is bounded by

    (a/ln2) F_li(A)+a log2(ell_l/s_l).

Summing gives |A|log2(6eM)+the rank bills+S(A). Size choices cost at most |A|log2 3. Child-count choices cost at most |A|(1+log2 3), since |F|<=|A| and sum c_l=|F|-|roots|<=|A|. Multisets cost at most 2|A|. Child gate choices cost exactly the child portion of L_lambda. Thus the probability of this one successful run supplies

    log2(1/Q1(A)) <= Lambda_lambda+(K0+log2 M)|A|
                     +S(A)+L_lambda(A)+sum_{j in A}G_j(A).       (5)

Set Q(empty)=1-v and Q(A)=v Q1(A) for A nonempty. Gibbs' inequality, with the empty atom handled exactly, gives

    H(f)<=h2(v)+sum_{A!=empty}p(A)log2(1/Q1(A)).

Also sum_{A:j in A}p(A)G_j(A)=alpha_j E_nu_j G_j by the derivative/Fourier identity. Average (5) and substitute (1). This proves (2)-(3), including v Lambda_lambda; no root normalizer has been dropped.

For a simple exact conservative constant, e<3, log2 e<3/2 (ln2>=2/3), log2 3<8/5 (3^5<2^8), and 6e<18<2^(9/2) give K0<22. Also A0<8 gives E0<(24/5)(3+3/2)=108/5. Hence K0+E0<218/5. We make no claim of sharp constants.

### 3. Explicit unbounded-read calibration with every charge bounded

For each integer a>=1 let m=2^a and

    f_a(x,y)=y_x,                 x in {0,1}^a, y in {-1,1}^m,
    h_u=1 iff (x=u and y_u=+1).

These m gates are regular oriented conjunctions, M=1, and their OR is f_a. Every selector belongs to m gates, so worst read k=m tends to infinity. Use the same desirability order in each conjunction: selectors in their common physical order, followed by its data coordinate. A Fourier set has exactly one data coordinate y_u and any selector subset T. For each selector j the rank expression is identical across all gates, while ell_u is one larger than ell_v for v!=u, so its rarity charge is no larger. Therefore assigning the entire A to u minimizes all G_j; choose u on ties. This gives a one-gate, one-root witness. Then S(A)=0 and L_1(A)=0 for every nonzero Fourier set. Note that the root's gate identity is paid through its independent root odds, not discarded.

Here pi_u=2^(-a-1), and

    Lambda_1=m log2(1+1/(2m)) <= (1/2)log2 e.

The function is balanced, v=1 and h2(v)=0. Its spectrum has exactly 2^(2a) coefficients of squared magnitude 2^(-2a), namely A=T union {y_u}. Thus H=2a, I=1+a/2; these identities are established controls, reproduced here rather than advertised as new class results.

For a data coordinate, there is one pivotal piece, so kappa=1. For a selector j, group the gates into neighboring address pairs u,v differing only at j. At a pivotal input exactly one of those two gates changes, and the two fine signed pieces are

    d_u = indicator(other address)*indicator(y_u=+1,y_v=-1),
    d_v = -indicator(other address)*indicator(y_u=-1,y_v=+1).

Up to the sign fixed by the j orientation their sum is

    D_{uv}=indicator(other address)*(y_u-y_v)/2.

Every nonzero Fourier index of D_{uv} contains exactly one of its two private data coordinates, hence different pairs have disjoint Fourier supports. The block criterion gives kappa_j=2, epsilon_j=0 for every selector, uniformly in a. Each selector has influence 1/2, so

    Gamma <= [(1/2)log2 e+(24/5)(a/2)]/(1+a/2)
           < 3/4+24/5.

Equation (3) gives H(f_a)<50 I(f_a) for all a. All label/root/subset/rank and normalizer costs are included. The original worst-read theorem gives 41+7a, which diverges; the new criterion is constant. The known exact H/I=4a/(a+2)<4 is stronger, and remains the preferred bound on addressing. The new information is the explicit bounded interference certificate within the complete queue criterion, not a new addressing theorem.

### 4. Exact PP tests and analytic obstruction to worst-event congestion

For prime q, use normalized nonzero triples over F_q as points and normal vectors; a line consists of triples orthogonal to its normal. There are n=q^2+q+1 points/lines, each line has q+1 points, each point lies on q+1 lines, and distinct lines meet once. PP_q is OR over all line conjunctions. Each line is a monotone minterm: on its own point-set assignment no other line is contained. Every monotone DNF representation therefore contains all lines, so the monotone-DNF minimum read is at least q+1. This assertion is deliberately limited to monotone conjunction covers.

Fix j. There are m=q+1 incident lines. Let A_l mean that all q other points of incident line l are positive; P(A_l)=2^(-q). These q-point sets are disjoint across the incident lines. Let B_r mean a nonincident line is all positive; there are q^2 such lines. The least-gate piece l is exactly A_l, none of the earlier A_t, and none of the B_r. For any t!=l,

    P(A_l intersect A_t)=2^(-2q).

Each nonincident line meets incident line l in one of its q other points, so its conjunction with A_l also has probability 2^(-2q). A union bound proves

    2^(-q)[1-(q^2+q)2^(-q)] <= alpha_jl <= 2^(-q).               (6)

The lower bound can be negative at small q; its asymptotic use is restricted to delta_q=(q^2+q)2^(-q)<1. All pieces are positive indicators. Hence their empty Fourier coefficients are alpha_jl and the sum's empty coefficient is alpha_j=sum_l alpha_jl. At this singleton Fourier event,

    nu_j(empty)/rho_j(empty)=alpha_j^2/sum_l alpha_jl^2
                    >= (q+1)(1-delta_q)^2 -> infinity.          (7)

Thus no uniform bound on the lead's worst-event kappa is possible for PP_q. This is an actual Boolean family refuting that congestion helper, not FEI. The equal-mass premise is false for the exact least-gate partition, but asymptotic equal masses make the obstruction stronger, not weaker.

Exact all-coordinate results:

| Family | I | least-gate masses for each coordinate (ordered) | max kappa |
|---|---|---|---|
| PP2 | 63/32 | 7/64, 3/32, 5/64 | 162/55 |
| PP3 | 5005/2048 | 107/2048, 199/4096, 185/4096, 43/1024 | 3698/925 |

PP2's maximum is realized at the empty residual Fourier index. PP3's empty ratio is 296450/74603, slightly below 3698/925: other events are also nearly maximally coherent. Exact epsilon(1), epsilon(2), rank expectation intervals and tail-event ratios are in `out/check.json`. For q=2,3 every nonempty incident intersection has ell>=1>b_l/gamma, so rarity is identically zero and the listed rank-only intervals are the full G statistic for the canonical sorted line order. This simplification is not extrapolated to large q.

### 5. L2 perturbation repair: PP rank term paid for every prime q

The failed zero-error helper admits a quantitative repair. The following general lemma does not require ideal pieces to have disjoint physical supports. Suppose ideal functions a_l have at most one nonzero Fourier coefficient at each index outside an exceptional index set E. For the actual disjoint physical pieces d_l put e_l=a_l-d_l, e=sum_l e_l, d=sum_l d_l and alpha=||d||_2^2. At T outside E, write a=sum_l a_l. Then

    dhat(T)^2 <= 2 ahat(T)^2+2 ehat(T)^2
              <= 4 sum_l dhat_l(T)^2
                   +4 sum_l ehat_l(T)^2+2 ehat(T)^2.              (8)

The second inequality follows because only one ideal amplitude is nonzero; for that index, |ahat_l|^2<=2|dhat_l|^2+2|ehat_l|^2. Consequently,

    epsilon(4) <= nu(E)+[4 sum_l ||e_l||_2^2+2||e||_2^2]/alpha.   (9)

Both the pointwise inequality and the L2 bill are explicit. No comparison of entropies of coherent and incoherent spectra is used.

For PP_q, let a_l=indicator(A_l) from section 4. Each a_l depends on its own q-point block; these blocks partition all points except j. Its Fourier coefficients vanish outside that block. Thus the ideal pieces have disjoint Fourier supports except E={empty}. The actual d_l is a subindicator of a_l. With m=q+1, D=q^2+q and delta=D2^(-q), the same union bound as (6) gives

    ||e_l||_2^2=P(A_l and not piece_l)<=D2^(-2q),
    sum_l ||e_l||_2^2<=mD2^(-2q),
    ||e||_2^2<=m sum_l ||e_l||_2^2<=m^2 D2^(-2q).

The middle pointwise Cauchy-Schwarz inequality holds even though the error supports overlap. Since nu(empty)=alpha, (9) and (6) yield, whenever delta<1,

    epsilon_j(4) <= alpha_j+(4+2m)D2^(-q)/(1-delta).             (10)

All rank orders have ranks at most q+1 and rarity charges at most b_l=q+1, so

    Gmax_j <= q+1+(6/5)log2(q+1).                               (11)

For every integer q>=16, delta<=1/2: at q=16 it is 272/65536, and q(q+1)2^(-q) decreases thereafter. Moreover m<=2q, D<=2q^2, 4+2m<=3q and alpha<=2q2^(-q). Equations (10)-(11) give

    epsilon_j(4)<=14q^3 2^(-q),
    epsilon_j(4)Gmax_j <= (308/5)q^4 2^(-q)<=308/5.              (12)

The last inequality is exact: 16^4/2^16=1 and the successive ratio ((q+1)/q)^4/2<1 for q>=16, since 17^4<2*16^4. For q<16, epsilon_j(4)<=1 and (11) gives Gmax_j<=104/5, so the same bound 308/5 holds. The small-q argument does not use a positive lower bound in (6).

Thus, for every prime q, every coordinate admits the **fixed choice kappa_j=4** and the all-dimensional paid bill

    sum_j alpha_j epsilon_j(4)Gmax_j <= (308/5)I(PP_q).

Substituting in (1), with M=1, gives

    sum_j alpha_j E_nu_j G_j <= [E0+356/5]I(PP_q) < 93 I(PP_q). (13)

The constant is conservative; (12) decays exponentially for large q. This proves a rank/rarity congestion replacement with unbounded actual read q+1 and bounded exceptional bill, even though zero-error worst-event congestion diverges. It uses spectral concentration of the **error of private-block ideal pieces**, not concentration of the masses of the actual pieces. The lead's equal-mass issue is therefore bypassed.

**First unsupported line for the complete PP_q theorem:** one fixed reference and its minimizing assignment have h2(v)+v Lambda_lambda+E[S+L_lambda]=O(I(PP_q)), with a uniform constant. Equation (13) pays the remaining rank term outright. The lead owns those root/label/subset estimates; they are not consumed as proved facts here. In particular, (13) alone is not a complete PP FEI theorem and does not close the universal conjecture.

# A014 stage 2 — bounded intersections, complete sparse payment, and local blocking repair

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Run `20261012T031856Z-fleet05-A014-overlap-beyond-worst-case-read`, B03/A014. Uniform finite cube, Boolean +/-1 outputs, Fourier entropy and code costs in bits, ordinary total influence. Full FEI and the unrestricted linear-hypergraph target remain OPEN. This stage extends and repairs the previous A014 interference mechanism rather than opening another unrelated broad candidate.

The new complete theorem is: for an OR of distinct nonempty positive conjunctions with widths w_l<=W and pairwise support intersections <=t, t>=1, define mu=sum_l 2^(-w_l), theta=2^t mu. If theta W<=1/16, then **H(f)<71 I(f)**, independently of worst read, gate count and dimension. A common global coordinate reversal preserves the statement. Arbitrary mixed literal orientations are not asserted. The condition uses incidence and gate activation data, not H or an assumed FEI inequality.

The local rank theorem is separate: after factoring all nonincident gates disjoint from the incident residual support into a common outside factor C_j, the local pressure theta_j in DERIVATION.md suffices for **R<47I** when theta_j W_j<=1/16 at every relevant coordinate. This has no global-density hypothesis, but does not pay the complete whole-set reference. Its coordinatewise-minimum assignment differs from the single whole-set owner in the complete sparse theorem.

Explicit unbounded-read example: the q^2 nonvertical affine-line conjunctions on F_q^2, for every prime q>=19, have width q and read q, with 2q^3 2^(-q)<=1/16. The theorem gives 71 against the original bound 41+7log2 q. We do not re-enumerate or reprove the lead's separate projective-plane result.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read BOARD snapshot 2026-10-12 03:24 UTC (relevant B02/B03 and review updates), B03 STREAM_NOTES v2 (23:2xZ), own STATUS and LEARNED, and execution protocol `fei-execution-protocol/2`, revision `2026-10-11-execution-v2`. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The full source-complete proof is saved immediately in [DERIVATION.md](DERIVATION.md), hash-bound in CLAIMS/receipts. Its dependency chain is:

1. For any Fourier decomposition d=sum_l u_l+r, projected Young's inequality retains the exact signed cross sum:

       ||P_E d||^2 <=(1+eta)[sum_l||P_Eu_l||^2+sum_{l!=m}<P_Eu_l,P_Eu_m>]
                        +(1+1/eta)||P_Er||^2.

   The same holds for a nonnegative Fourier-cost multiplier. No cross term is discarded for an arbitrary union.

2. For positive AND gates at coordinate j, let S_j count satisfied incident residual clauses and B_j the event of a satisfied nonincident clause. Then d_jf=1[S_j>0]1[not B_j]. Writing a_j=sum_incident 2p_l, pairwise intersection estimates yield

       C_j=E[S_j(S_j-1)]<=2^(t-1)a_j^2,
       E[S_j 1[B_j]]<=2^t a_j(mu-a_j/2),
       ||d_jf-S_j||^2<=theta a_j,
       Inf_j(f)>=(1-theta)a_j.

   Thus Q=sum_j a_j=2sum_l w_l p_l and I>=(1-theta)Q. The ideal cross spectrum is nonnegative and has exact total mass C_j. Nonnegative products, rather than nonnegative individual FWHT amplitudes in every bit convention, are the required property.

3. A good Fourier set A is contained in some gate. Assign it wholly to the gate minimizing SUM_j G_jl(A). Its ideal diagonal weights (2p_l)^2 are identical across j in A, so the minimizing owner's total cost is at most the weighted average over owners. For a bad set all ideal derivative amplitudes vanish. With Gmax<=(11/5)W, the published piece tails and multiplier lemma give

       R<=[2E0+(44/5)theta W]Q,
       E_p[|A|1[bad]]<=theta Q.

   This averaged whole-set argument is not a claim that one owner minimizes each coordinate separately.

4. Use one fixed queue with root odds p_l and child-gate prior p_l/sum_incident p_m. Root normalization is Lambda=sum_l log2(1+p_l)<=mu log2 e. A child name costs <=w_l<=W, because mu<1. Good sets need one root and no child/subset congestion. Bad sets cost <=(11/5)W|A| in additional names and subset allocation, using log2 N<=W. Dense root rarity and sparse root rarity are paid in the original forest manner; all elementary costs contribute K0 I. Consequently

       H<=h2(v)+v Lambda+K0 I+L+S+R,
       L+S<=(11/5)theta W Q.

   Gibbs uses Q(empty)=1-v and Q(A)=v Q1(A). The term is v Lambda, not Lambda erased or an independently optimized normalizer.

5. Use h2(v)<=2I, v<=4mu, Q>=2mu, K0<22, E0<108/5. The exact final expression is

       H/I <24+[2E0+11theta W+3mu]/(1-theta)
            <24+[216/5+11/16+3/32]*(16/15)
            =10637/150<71.

6. The raw approximation has an exact dense read-one obstruction: on m disjoint width-w clauses,

       ||d_jf-u_j||^2/Inf_j=(1-2^(-w))^(-(m-1))-1 -> infinity.

   These functions already satisfy C003 with k=1. This refutes a density-free normalized raw-remainder helper, not FEI. Factor distant false-clause constraints into C_j. Its independence from the incident residual variables and decreasing/increasing covariance induction pay only nearby blockers. The exact local pressure theta_j in DERIVATION yields

       E_nu_j G_j <=[2E0+4theta_j Gmax_j]/(1-theta_j),
       R<(140/3)I<47I if all theta_j W_j<=1/16.

**First unsupported line for the lead's unrestricted target:** a whole-set reference can pay the spectra of these common outside factors and shared blocking once, together with actual roots/labels/subset costs, when global pressure 2^t mu W is unbounded. Neither the complete sparse theorem nor the local rank theorem establishes that line. A dimension-independent O(t) intersection payment beyond the present 2^t activation condition is also unproved. No FEI disproof or unconditional all-linear-hypergraph theorem is claimed.

# A015 / B03: minterm label barrier, with activation normalization

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every indicated finite Boolean family, quantify the charged label component in the Section 5 queue witness at the **fixed scale lambda=1/k**:

`C_lab(f,D) = sum_{A != empty} fhat(A)^2 min_tau |F_tau(A)| log2 k`.

Here D is a specified DNF, R_l is the physical support of term l, tau assigns every j in A to one term containing j, and F_tau is the set of used terms. Every such assignment has the source-component spanning forest required by Proposition 5.5. Define separately the allocation pressure `P_sub(A,tau)=sum_l s_l log2(|A intersect R_l|/s_l)`, whose coefficient in the proof is 6/5. Empty sets cost zero. All expectations below are **unconditioned** squared-Fourier expectations, and all logarithms are in bits. Conditional expectations given nonempty A must be multiplied by v=Var(f).

Two DNF conventions must be distinguished. FULL CELLS means one full-width signed conjunction for every input in the true event. PRIME means the canonical minimal positive conjunctions for the monotone examples. Calling both 'minterms' hides the tribes contrast.

Main outcome: fixed-scale labels already obstruct a dimension-independent payment for majority, even after optimal whole-set gate assignment. Prime tribes has zero label charge. Sparse activation does not make the majority barrier disappear. These are coding-component obstructions, not FEI counterexamples or lower bounds on every optimized reference. One bundled scoped diagnostic is submitted for review; no new universal representation lemma is asserted.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Execution protocol `fei-execution-protocol/2`, revision `2026-10-11-execution-v2`, read in full; README, CURRENT_STATUS, CLAIM_REGISTRY, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY, PACKET_STATUS and STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS inspected. Plan source: B03/A015 and its execution contract, plus A016/A021/A076 interfaces. B03 STREAM_NOTES v1 (2026-10-11 22:30Z) and BOARD (2026-10-11 22:57 UTC) direct this work to the three terms (L),(S),(R). No shared files were changed.

The 38-page threshold TXT has SHA256 `36a5953075759ea1085b005cb92352d364693c45a37fc35260d3b39f21cde20c`. Read its regular-function definition, Theorem A, Section 5 (especially equations 31,32,35, Proposition 5.5 and the fixed-scale refinement), and Proposition 8.9 / activation-equivalence corollary; verified these interfaces against supplemental `baseline/current-threshold-paper.tex`. The earlier 29-page TXT (`056e86b12567f3ba766d4aaeb8beb97042151eee3094c1bcd0d27e6ca32a1007`) was inspected for theorem/version differences, not used for joint-witness or activation claims. The percolation 30-page TXT (`d33469ce69f21137a9620927df39c33ff65ef45f54f8be2fec8a55210d46c8b6`) and other 31-page version (`4f09cf6e96559f5c7bbbe67e4f15ba34017770fc498f4bc178c928419189e00b`) were inspected at their theorem/entropy interfaces; no percolation theorem is a proof dependency here. Those papers' spatial hypotheses do not pay arbitrary DNF labels. This is a targeted source intake, not a claim to have independently audited every proof in those papers.

Supplemental CAMPAIGN, CLAIMS, COUNTEREXAMPLES, SEED_LEMMAS and NATIVE_MAPPING were consulted, particularly C008/C013/C020, the complete-reference prior lemma, and X011/X012. Source collection headings and the overlap-product correction were inspected. Earlier B05/B09/B19 run reports were located and their scopes inspected; no new proof depends on their unreviewed claims. A076's existing report is the threshold/tribes baseline: entropy formulas below are calibration, not another claimed discovery of tribes FEI. The new delta is their comparison with the minterm label/allocation charges. Source hashes and intake locators are retained in `out/SOURCES.json`.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. The fixed-scale component and why it is not a universal lower bound

At lambda=1/k, each forest root contributes log2 k as part of `log2(1/(lambda*pi_l))`; every child label is bounded above by log2 k. Thus the explicit refined bound charges exactly the formal component `|F| log2 k`, because roots plus edges equals |F|. The actual child contribution is log2 d(j_e), which can be smaller. This report minimizes the formal component over complete assignments. It does not replace it by the weaker coordinatewise `|A| log2 k`.

The complete law also has `h2(v)+v Lambda_lambda`, root rarity, subset normalizers/ranks, size choices, and attachments. It can sum probabilities of different successful witnesses. A lower bound on this **charged expression** is not a lower bound on `log2(1/Q(A))`, on entropy, or on all scales. Changing scale or erasing labels is permissible only in the full cost. The first unsupported line of any proposed universal conclusion would be: 'for every Boolean f there exists a complete normalized representation/reference whose expected total cost is <=K I(f)'. Nothing here proves it.

### 2. Majority: the label obstruction survives whole-set assignment

Let n=2a+1, t=a+1, a>=0; the true event is at least t positive bits. Its prime DNF has one term for each t-subset, each support of size t. The number of terms is N=binom(n,t), and the exact read is k=binom(n-1,t-1). These terms are mandatory in any exact DNF in the following sense: at the input whose positive set is exactly a given t-set, any satisfied implicant must require all t of those positive literals. Otherwise turn every unconstrained positive literal negative and obtain a false input satisfying that term. Distinct t-sets therefore require distinct terms. Each coordinate occurs in at least binom(n-1,t-1) terms in every DNF, even allowing negative literals or redundant terms. Such additions cannot make the formal fixed-scale label component less than log2 k because every nonempty spectral set needs at least one used term.

For the canonical prime DNF, the exact optimal count for a set A of size d is `ceil(d/t)`. The lower bound is support size. For the upper bound, partition A into groups of size at most t and extend each to a t-set; all extended groups are available terms. Since n<2t, this is one for d<=t and two for d>t. Thus, writing W_d=P(|S|=d) and T_n=sum_{d>t} W_d,

`E min |F| = 1+T_n`,

`C_lab(Maj_n,prime) = (1+T_n) log2 binom(n-1,t-1)`.

This is exact, not a bound using E|S|. Only odd levels have mass. A completely explicit spectrum is provided by

`b_d = 2^(-n) sum_{j=0}^n sign(j-t) sum_h (-1)^h binom(d,h) binom(n-d,j-h)`,

where sign(j-t) is +1 for j>=t and -1 otherwise (overall sign/input reversals do not affect W). Then `W_d=binom(n,d)b_d^2`. Equivalently, put `c_j=binom(2j,j)/4^j`; for d=2j+1,

`W_(2j+1) = [n/(2j+1)] c_a c_j c_(a-j)` (0<=j<=a).

One derivation of this last formula: for a chosen coordinate, the majority derivative is the indicator that the other 2a bits have exactly a positives. Its coefficient on 2j chosen bits is `(-1)^j c_a binom(a,j)/binom(2a,2j)`, obtained by taking the coefficient of x^a in `(1-x)^(2j)(1+x)^(2a-2j)` and dividing by 2^(2a). Squaring and multiplying by binom(n,2j+1), followed by factorial cancellation, gives the displayed W. The convolution identity `sum_j c_j c_(a-j)=1` then yields

`I(Maj_n)=n c_a = n binom(n-1,t-1)/2^(n-1)`.

Markov gives `T_n<=I/t=O(n^(-1/2))`. Wallis/Stirling gives `I~sqrt(2n/pi)` and `log2 k=n-1-(1/2)log2 n+O(1)`. Consequently

`C_lab/I ~ sqrt(pi*n/2) -> infinity`.

Actual entropy is exactly `H(W)+sum_d W_d log2 binom(n,d)`, and is Theta(sqrt(n)). The upper bound is baseline Theorem A, H<=C_reg I. For completeness a crude analytic lower bound avoids numerical extrapolation: c_l>=1/(2sqrt(l)) for l>=1 (square and induct using c_(l+1)/c_l=(2l+1)/(2l+2)). For a>=8 and integers ceil(a/4)<=j<=floor((a-1)/2), W_(2j+1)>=1/(8a^(3/2)); there are at least a/8 such j. Their degrees satisfy a/2<=d<=a and log2 binom(n,d)>=d>=a/2, since binom(n,d)>=(n/d)^d and n/d>=2. Therefore H>=sqrt(a)/128. The growing label/influence ratio is a code artifact while H/I stays bounded.

The allocation pressure is not the main obstruction. Among assignments using the minimum number of terms, its minimum is zero for every d<n. For d<=t use a term containing A. For t<d<n, split A into two parts with sizes each at least `d-t+1` and at most t, then fill both terms with coordinates outside A; their intersections with A are exactly their assigned parts. Such a split exists since d<=2t-2. For d=n every support intersection is t. Concavity of `s log2(t/s)` shows the minimum over partitions with s<=t is attained by sizes t,t-1: transferring size toward an endpoint cannot increase the sum. Thus

`E min P_sub = W_n (t-1) log2(t/(t-1)) <= W_n log2 e = O(1/n)`

(n=1 gives zero separately). The subsets giving minimum pressure also have minimum |F|. In contrast the generic bound is I log2 k. Neither allocation nor paying the same name once per whole set removes majority's remaining fixed-scale label bill. The generic (R) estimate still has `(24/5)I log2 k`; this report does not pay that term in this DNF.

The full-cell DNF has N=k=2^(n-1), all supports [n], and a one-gate assignment for every A. Its exact formal label charge is n-1; allocation pressure is zero. A direct n-bit characteristic-vector subset code would add a dimension bill; a size-first uniform code has cost `log2(d(d+1))+log2 binom(n,d)`. Its expected binomial term is already the conditional entropy in the displayed symmetric entropy identity. Full-cell naming is therefore distinct from coding the output set.

### 3. Tribes: prime supports remove the label term

Let T_(w,s) be the OR of s disjoint width-w all-positive tribes, w,s>=1. Put z=2^(-w), q=1-(1-z)^s and choose Boolean sign g=1-2T. Its canonical prime DNF has s disjoint supports and k=1. For a spectral set A, write J(A) for the number of hit tribes. Each hit tribe is mandatory and sufficient, so `min |F|=J(A)`; all gate-owned subsets have `|A intersect R_l|=s_l`. Therefore

`C_lab=0`, `P_sub=0`,

`E J=4s z(1-z)^s`, `I(g)=2sw z(1-z)^(s-1)`.

To verify the expectation, write `g=2 product_l (1-A_l)-1`. Every nonempty coefficient on a set hitting j tribes equals `2(-z)^j(1-z)^(s-j)` up to oriented signs, with multiplicity `binom(s,j)(2^w-1)^j`. Summing its squared mass gives v=4q(1-q); summing hit counts gives E J. The empty coefficient is 1-2q. This proves the normalization and count formulas directly.

The exact entropy is

`H = (1-2q)^2 log2(1/(1-2q)^2) - 2v + 2w E J + 2 log2(1/(1-z)) (s v-E J)`

with 0 log(1/0)=0. In the standard regime w->infinity, sz->lambda in (0,infinity), I~2lambda exp(-lambda)w and H/I->4 (A076 baseline). No entropy asymptotic is needed for the label obstruction.

The full-cell DNF instead has `N=k=2^(ws)-(2^w-1)^s=2^(ws)q` terms, all support [ws]. Consequently `E|F|=v`, `C_lab=v log2 N`, allocation pressure zero. In the same regime, `C_lab/I ~ 2(1-exp(-lambda))s/lambda -> infinity`. This large spurious charge disappears completely on passing to the prime DNF. A whole-set single-name code alone therefore suffices for neither representation: first the right structural supports must be chosen.

### 4. Arbitrary rare activated cores: exact mass, influence and dimension charge

Let g be any Boolean function on m declared data bits, q=P(g=-1)>0, and let A_r be a point indicator on r>=1 fresh controls, p=2^(-r). Define F=1+A_r(g-1). For data B!=empty and arbitrary control U, `Fhat(B,U)=p epsilon_U ghat(B)`. For empty data and U!=empty it is `-2pq epsilon_U`; the empty coefficient is 1-2pq. Thus

`v_F=4pq(1-pq)`, `I(F)=p(I(g)+2rq)`.

The nonempty-data sector has mass p Var(g), not p^2 Var(g): there are 2^r control sets. The control-only nonempty sector has mass `4p q^2(1-p)`. This is the normalization error to avoid when importing sparse-core intuition.

Full-cell terms for the activated event consist of its one fixed control point and one of the `N_-=2^m q` negative core assignments; every support is [r+m], so k=N_-, `min|F|=1` on nonempty A. Exactly

`C_lab(F,full cells)=4pq(1-pq) log2(2^m q)`,

`C_lab/I(F)=4q(1-pq) log2(2^m q)/(I(g)+2rq)`.

For balanced cores this becomes `(2-p)(m-1)/(I(g)+r)`. Allocation pressure is zero. Subset ranks, root rarity `(m+r)` per root, and normalization remain outside this label calculation.

For comparison with the actual target, if balanced,

`H(F)=p H(g)+2rp(2-p)+(1-p)^2 log2(1/(1-p)^2)`.

This is Proposition 8.9, independently checked coefficient by coefficient by the pilot. For general q the direct exact expression is

`H(F)=p H^+(g)+2rp Var(g)+4p q^2(1-p) log2(1/(4p^2 q^2))+(1-2pq)^2 log2(1/(1-2pq)^2)`.

No balanced approximation is used for the 810 arbitrary-core activation cases.

In particular take an m-bit odd majority core and r=ceil(2log2(m+1)). Then p->0, minority probability p/2->0, I(F)->0, but the full-cell charge/influence ratio is Theta(sqrt(m)). Its actual H/I stays bounded by the regular-gate theorem below. If r>>m the displayed label ratio vanishes; r cannot be silently omitted in classifying extreme regimes. Padding a fixed balanced core with dummy data bits increases this full-cell label charge linearly in padding while H(g) and I(g) remain unchanged; this makes the representation dependence unmistakable.

For the prime activated-majority DNF the supports are all r controls plus a t=(m+1)/2-subset of data. Every control occurs in N=binom(m,t) terms, and each data coordinate in binom(m-1,t-1); hence k=N. For a nonempty spectral set `(B,U)`, the exact minimum count is `max(1,ceil(|B|/t))`. Summing the two sectors gives

`E min|F|=2p-p^2+p T_m`,

`C_lab/I(F)=(2-p+T_m) log2 binom(m,t)/(I(Maj_m)+r)`.

Again this diverges as Theta(sqrt(m)) for the sparse choice r=ceil(2log2(m+1)). Assign all U controls to the first of the at most two used terms. Using the preceding majority data partition, pressure is zero for |B|<=t, at most r log2 e for t<|B|<m, and at most (r+1)log2 e at |B|=m. Thus `E P_sub<=p T_m(r+1)log2 e`. This is an upper bound for an explicit minimum-count assignment, not a claim of exact minimum activated pressure. The surviving label obstruction is not created by excessive subset congestion.

For completeness, activate tribes using their prime terms. If J(B)>0 the count is J(B), and if B=empty,U!=empty it is one. Controls have read s, data have read one, so k=s. Exactly

`E min|F|=4p q^2(1-p)+4p s z(1-z)^s`,

`C_lab/I(F)=[4q^2(1-p)+4s z(1-z)^s] log2 s/[2sw z(1-z)^(s-1)+2rq]`.

For sz->lambda, r->infinity and r=o(w), this ratio tends to `2[ (1-exp(-lambda))^2+lambda exp(-lambda)]/[lambda exp(-lambda)]`. More generally if r/w->rho>=0 and p->p_* it tends to `4[(1-exp(-lambda))^2(1-p_*)+lambda exp(-lambda)]/[2lambda exp(-lambda)+2rho(1-exp(-lambda))]`; log2(s)/w->1. Fixed r must retain p_*=2^(-r); rho>0 forces p_*=0. It stays bounded in these regimes, unlike the full-cell representation. Assign controls to one hit tribe. Other hit tribes contribute `b_l log2(1+|U|/b_l)<=|U| log2 e` each, giving `E P_sub<=p r log2(e) E_g J`. This explicit residual bound does not purport to pay all activated-tribes rank/normalization costs for arbitrary parameters.

### 5. Structural grouping repairs majority; a universal grouping lemma is still unpaid

Majority is a single regular gate. Activated majority is also regular: orient the event as increasing, put all controls before all data, and regard coordinates within each group as equivalent. If two inputs differ only by moving a 1 from data to a control, either another control is 0 and both outputs are false, or the input with this control 0 is false while the other is possibly true. Thus every control is at least as desirable as every data bit; data are exchangeable under the threshold. This is a proof of regularity, independently checked in the pilot. A one-gate representation has k=M=1, |F|=1, and zero (L) and allocation pressure. Theorem A pays the remaining complete entropy with C_reg<8.937. These are baseline gate-class consequences, not a new full-FEI case claim.

The precise missing feature in majority's fixed-scale DNF charge is **quotienting interchangeable minterms into a structural gate, or obtaining an equivalent posterior refund with all rarity/normalization bills retained**. The exact unpaid formal bill is `(1+T_n)log2 k`, already Theta(n), while its influence is Theta(sqrt(n)). Merely paying each label once per set improves I log k to this bill and still fails. Tribes shows that smaller physical supports can supply the needed grouping. Arbitrary cores do not come with a regularity order, cheap grouped gates, or a proved posterior refund.

An important algebraic sanity check: if N full-cell terms have the same support, same chosen rank order, and equal pi=2^(-d), the N distinct one-root/no-child runs selecting a fixed output subset have identical non-root factors. Their summed root mass is `N * 2^(-Lambda_lambda) lambda pi`. At lambda=1/N this cancels the explicit log2 N label charge exactly. The remaining factor is pi: the root rarity still costs d, in addition to Lambda and the subset factors. This is a valid finite multiplicity refund, not a universal proof or an assertion that optimal posterior cost must still be >=d. It explains why no raw-label barrier can be promoted to a lower bound on the complete optimized law. A fixed scale prior, root term, subset normalizers and posterior entropy must be assessed together as in C008 and the seed complete-reference lemma.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Independently accepted activation repair; complete core debt survives

`A012_REVIEW.md` gives a reconstruction of the entire A012 v1 claim without importing its code or repairing its proof. For balanced core g and a fixed nonempty spectral reference R_g of mass z<=1 and cost c_g, use

`Q_F(empty,empty)=(1-p)^2`,

`Q_F(U,empty)=p^2` for U nonempty,

`Q_F(U,T)=p^2 R_g(T)` for T nonempty and every U,

where p=2^-r and F=1+A_r(g-1) on fresh controls. Exact summation gives reference mass `1-p+pz` and

`CE(P_F,Q_F)=p c_g+A_r`,

`A_r=2rp(2-p)+(1-p)^2 log2(1/(1-p)^2)`,

`D(P_F||Q_F)=p D(P_g||R_g)`.

The true control-first witness satisfies the original posterior formula in A012. Its physical stopping label J has H(J)=2(1-p), and W is deterministically equivalent to (J,S). The posterior is independent of the balanced core and tends to two bits. Original scope, sharp selector coefficient, and disjoint-reference iteration all hold. The coefficient is uniquely maximized at r=3:

`C_act=15/4+(49/12)log2(8/7)`, with `4.536<C_act<4.537`.

The first unsupported universal line remains `c_g<=K I(g)` for one finite K for all balanced cores, or a genuinely new paid shared-control/colliding-child transfer. The activation theorem preserves arbitrary seed cost; it does not construct that missing core reference.

### 2. Exact full-minterm root cancellation, including conditioning

Let f be any nonconstant Boolean function on n>=1 declared physical coordinates. Its positive full-cell DNF has N>=1 distinct terms, all support [n], all density `pi=2^-n`. Choose the fixed admissible gate weights lambda_l=1/N, and the same rank order in every term. Its independent-root normalization is

`Lambda=N log2(1+pi/N)`, `Z=1-2^-Lambda`, `D=2^Lambda Z=(1+pi/N)^N-1`.

Condition on a nonempty initial root set. The probability of **exactly one** root in that conditioned experiment is

`eta=pi/D`.

Indeed each selected one-root set has unconditional probability `2^-Lambda pi/N`; summing the N distinct actual runs and dividing by Z gives pi/D. The non-root subset laws are identical for these full supports and common orders, regardless of the literal signs. Their probabilities may therefore be summed before taking a logarithm.

This is a stronger refund than stage 1's unconditioned root sum. In that sum the minterm name disappears but pi remains. Conditioning contributes `Lambda+log Z=log D`, canceling that root rarity as well, up to the exact factor eta. Binomial expansion gives `D>=pi`; also `D<=(exp(pi)-1)<=pi exp(pi)`, so

`exp(-pi)<=eta<=1`, `0<=-log2 eta<=pi log2 e`.

For the finite rational checks, the cruder elementary bound `D<=pi/(1-pi)` gives `eta>=1-pi`. The case N=1 gives eta=1 exactly. No sample-dependent scale is selected. No rank, subset or termination cost has been canceled by this root computation.

### 3. Explicit output references and the role of the order prior

To keep every finite reference probability rational, use rank exponent **two**, rather than the original forest exponent 6/5. This is an explicitly modified valid output reference, not a claim about the original PI-J infimum. For each size s, give coordinate j weight `theta_s(j)=min(1,(s/rank(j))^2)` and normalize s-subsets by their elementary symmetric polynomial `Z_s`. Choose s with probability `1/[s(s+1)]`; an invalid size fails. Require no children, an event of probability one half. Keep only the conditioned one-root/no-child finite runs; all other mass is discarded as failure. Their complete output reference is

`R_ord(A)=eta prod_{j in A} theta_s(j)/[2s(s+1)Z_s]`, `s=|A|>=1`.

Its mass is exactly `eta n/[2(n+1)]`. Thus even the surviving finite-run failure cost is present. Adding vacuum probability 1-v and multiplying nonempty output mass by v is a complete subprobability with explicit empty-sector CE; v=Var(f). The examples below are balanced, so v=1.

Alternatively choose the common order uniformly from all n! permutations **before sampling**. This is a fixed normalized order prior; its output probabilities average the complete references, and no order-selection cost is omitted from that average. For each size s the averaged local subset law is uniform, by permutation equivariance and normalization. Hence exactly

`R_sym(A)=eta/[2s(s+1)binom(n,s)]`,

`CE(P,R_sym)=-log2 eta+1+E log2[|S|(|S|+1)]+E log2 binom(n,|S|)`

for a balanced spectrum. This shows explicitly how root names and latent orders are erased, while the physical output subset still has to be named. The same symmetrization identity holds for any exponent alpha>1, including the original 6/5; exponent two is used for rational construction and the ordered-cost certificate below.

### 4. All exchangeable references: exact optimization and a stronger barrier

Let Q be any subprobability on subsets, invariant under all coordinate permutations. Write W_s=P(|S|=s), and let q_s be its total mass at size s. Then `Q(A)=q_s/binom(n,s)`. If it vanishes on actual support its CE is infinite. Otherwise

`CE(P,Q)=sum_s W_s log2(1/q_s)+sum_s W_s log2 binom(n,s)`.

Minimizing over all such subprobabilities gives the exact optimum at q_s=W_s:

`inf_exchangeable CE=H(W)+E log2 binom(n,|S|)`.

Proof: q_s sum to at most one, so subprobability Gibbs gives the first term >=H(W), with equality for q=W. The excess over actual entropy is precisely

`J_ex=sum_s W_s D(P(. | |S|=s) || Uniform_s)`

`=H(W)+E log2 binom(n,|S|)-H(P)`.

This is a location/conditional-shape cost, not the entropy of a gate name. It is nonnegative. The infimum already optimizes every possible size law, including empty mass. Optimizing a root scale, a size prior, or any posterior whose final output reference remains exchangeable cannot avoid it.

Take r>=1 fresh control bits, p=2^-r, and two data bits y,z. Define the genuine balanced rare-switch function

`M_r=y+A_r(z-y)`, on `n=r+2` coordinates.

Its Fourier coefficients are `1-p` on {y}, `-p epsilon_U` on {y}+U for U nonempty, and `p epsilon_U` on {z}+U for every U. Therefore all non-leading supported sets have mass p^2, and

`I(M_r)=1+rp`,

`Inf_y=1-p`, `Inf_z=p`, `Inf_control=p` for every control,

`H(M_r)=(1-p)^2 log2(1/(1-p)^2)+2r(2p-p^2) ->0`.

Every coordinate is relevant; there are no dummy variables in this obstruction. Singleton mass is

`W_1=(1-p)^2+p^2 ->1`.

Thus every exchangeable subprobability satisfies

`CE(P_Mr,Q)/I(M_r) >= W_1 log2(r+2)/(1+rp) ->infinity`.

More precisely the best exchangeable cost is `log2(r+2)+o(1)`. The tail degree mass is <=2p, its binomial cost is <=2pn, and `H(W)<=h2(2p)+2p log2 n` for r>=2; all tend to zero. The dominant singleton term differs from log2 n by o(1). Consequently `J_ex=log2(r+2)+o(1)`, although H/I->0. This refutes influence payment for the entire exchangeable-reference class, not FEI or a function-adapted reference.

### 5. A location-aware common order repairs the same family

For the rare switch use the fixed order y first, followed by z and all controls. This is decreasing influence; y has influence 1-p and every other coordinate has p. With exponent-two weights, elementary symmetric normalization obeys

`sum_j theta_s(j)<=2s`, `Z_s<=(2e)^s`.

For j in A, its occupied predecessor count is at most s-1, so

`-log2 theta_s(j)<=2 log2[rank(j)/(|A intersect predecessors_j|+1)]`.

Writing G_bits(A) for the sum of these logarithmic rank discrepancies, the complete ordered cost obeys

`CE(P,R_ord)<=-log2 eta+1+log2(6e) I+2 E G_bits`.

Discarding the nonnegative predecessor subtraction gives

`E G_bits<=sum_j Inf_j log2 rank(j)=p log2(n!)`.

The sequence `2^-r log2((r+2)!)` is nonincreasing for r>=1, because `(r+2)!>=r+3`. Thus `2p log2(n!)<=log2 6`. Since pi=2^-n<=1/8 and I>=1,

`CE(P_Mr,R_ord) <= [log2(6e)+log2 6+1+(1/8)log2 e] I(M_r)`

`< (639/80) I(M_r) <8 I(M_r)`.

The last strict certificate uses only e<3, ln2>2/3, `18^5<2^21` and `6^5<2^13`: the bracket is less than `21/5+13/5+1+3/16=639/80`. The pilot independently constructs the complete rational reference and verifies the cost bounds. This is a repair of the same output-location obstruction; it is not presented as a new class-FEI theorem. Actual H/I already tends to zero.

This order is not a universal remedy. B02's existing single-gate tribes obstruction shows a dimension-sized rank cost in every single-gate table of the specified model, even with its best orders. This stage consumes that obstruction rather than reproducing it. Majority needs symmetry or a paid regular rank law, rare switches need physical location awareness, and tribes needs block support information. Root cancellation alone supplies none of these choices universally.

### 6. Concrete grouped-majority transfer with failure mass and full core CE

Use a single regular majority gate with fixed physical desirability order. Define its nonempty core reference R_g by the exponent-two subset sampler above, with one mandatory root (so eta=1), and the no-child factor one half. Its mass is `z=n/[2(n+1)]`; it is **not** assumed to be a probability law or an exact spectral oracle.

The source-complete regular rank mean gives `E G_bits<=(1+log2 e)I(g)`. Therefore for a balanced regular core,

`c_g<=1+[log2(6e)+2(1+log2 e)] I(g)`

`<=[3+log2 6+3log2 e] I(g) = K_ord I(g)`.

The last step uses I(g)>=1 by Poincare for balanced Boolean g. This is an explicit complete core reference cost, not an inference from H<=C I to a free code. Its constant satisfies `K_ord<101/10`. Feed this R_g into the independently accepted A012 construction. The full new reference has mass `1-p+pz`, and

`CE_F=p c_g+A_r <=max(K_ord,C_act) p(I(g)+r)`.

Keeping the exact K_ord is valid; since K_ord> C_act, it yields CE_F<=K_ord I(F). Thus the promised stage-1 transfer to grouped activated majority is now concrete and source-bound. It does not use A006's pending PI-J majority theorem or establish anything about that separate infimum. The source rank law and the imported activation transfer are exactly the mathematical hypotheses paying the costs.

The check constructs core and activation output references for n=1,3,5,7,9 and r=1,2,3,4. It verifies actual parent Walsh spectra, failure masses, interval CE bounds, and the exact generalized-divergence identity by aggregating equal rational density ratios. The full core bill is multiplied by p, rather than discarded.

### 7. Exact remaining gap and nonduplication

The original raw-label majority barrier remains correct as a component of the fixed-scale unrefunded Theorem R estimate. It cannot be promoted to a no-go for the conditioned posterior infimum: A006 already provides a majority repair, and the full-minterm sum here cancels labels/root rarity explicitly. B03 v2's 'false for DNF of majority (labels)' should be read at that coarse-component scope.

The stronger universal temptation is now also ruled out: after summing all interchangeable minterm names, making the output law permutation-invariant does not produce a universal influence-paid representation. Its unpaid exact term is J_ex. Ordering by influence repairs the rare-switch example but is not a universal gate-cover or rank theorem. First unsupported line: for every Boolean f there is a fixed function-adapted support/order/reference mixture with complete posterior cost O(I), retaining terminal entropy and all normalization/model-selection charges. Neither exact activation transfer nor the current single-order/symmetric references prove that line.

# A015 stage 3: actual prime-witness erasure preserves physical locations

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Stream B03, packet A015, gap G-J-COST / C020. Entropy and costs are bits; uniform Boolean functions have values +/-1. This stage independently reconstructs the whole unchanged A006-ACTUAL-FOREST-DETERMINANT-MOMENT-BOUNDS@1 interface and consumes it on signed prime tables for the packet's rare switch M_r=y+A_r(z-y), for every integer r>=1.     Z_tau(u)>=2^(-(gamma-1)u H(a))>=k^(-u/5).

    k^(-u/5)<=Z_tau(u)<=k^(-u/5)exp((6/5)u^2/L).

    W_(k,sizes)=binom(N,k)N^(-k)(s)_(k-1)
                     [s!/prod_l s_l!] prod_l(s_l/s)^((6/5)s_l),

Gate IDs are chosen in binom(N,k) ways and ordered by the fixed table, so there is no extra k!. This retains label depletion, original root/chronological mass and congestion. It is a law of the surrogate base posterior, not the actual queue posterior.

# A016: addressing benchmark and code-cost ledger

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every integer a >= 0, set N=2^a and let f(x,y)=y_x on a fresh uniform selector block x in {-1,1}^a and N distinct uniform data bits y_u. Reproduce the spectrum, H and I, and compare explicitly defined coordinatewise, complete-set one-root, and conditioned posterior costs as a tends to infinity. Entropy and costs are in bits; I is ordinary total influence. This is a baseline reproduction, not a new FEI theorem. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

The execution protocol was read in full: fei-execution-protocol/2, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2. Also consulted the campaign README, CURRENT_STATUS, CLAIM_REGISTRY, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY, PACKET_STATUS, STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS and FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex, especially Endpoint J and A016's acceptance contract. BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-11 21:38 UTC has no completed B04 baseline. The requested claude/B04/STREAM_NOTES.md did not exist when checked; no lead direction was overridden.

Supplement: CAMPAIGN.md, CLAIMS.md (C008,C009,C020,C035), COUNTEREXAMPLES.md (X012), SEED_LEMMAS.md (complete-reference prior mixture), NATIVE_MAPPING.md, and sources/LOG_ALLOCATION_CURATED.md. Their baseline addressing statements are reused, not presented as novel.

Primary mathematical sources: context/threshold_paper_38pp.txt, Sections 5.2, 5.6 (Proposition 5.5), 5.7 and Section 8's affine-selector addressing example; source_material/reductions/paper/forests.tex, equations forestcost/posteriorforest/outerfractional/addresspressure and Proposition addressforest. The former allows a global scalar root odds parameter lambda=1, while the conditioned forest uses incidence weights lambda_b=1/N. These are different generators and are not silently conflated. The earlier threshold 29-page version's introductory definitions were checked; it is not the source for the later addressing refinement. The percolation 30-page and other 31-page versions' introductory spectral normalization and scope statements were checked; no spatial/percolation theorem is used here. Earlier B05, B19 and B09 run reports were checked for their scopes; none is a proof dependency of this computation. Exact local file hashes are in out/SOURCE_RECEIPTS.json.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Exact Boolean spectrum, including a=0

The selector indicator expands as

    1{x=u} = N^{-1} product_j (1+u_j x_j).

Therefore

    f = N^{-1} sum_u sum_{T subset [a]} chi_T(u) chi_T(x) y_u.

The Fourier sets T union {y_u} are distinct because the data coordinates are distinct and disjoint from the selector block. There are N^2 such sets, each coefficient has magnitude 1/N, and all other coefficients vanish. Thus the spectral law is the independent pair U uniform on N labels and T uniform on 2^a selector subsets. Consequently

    H=2a,  |S|=1+Bin(a,1/2),  I=1+a/2,  H=4(I-1).

Each selector has influence 1/2: flipping it selects two independent fair data bits, which disagree with probability 1/2. Each data coordinate has influence 1/N because it matters exactly at its own address. At a=0, f=y_0, H=0, I=1. The ratio H/I=4a/(a+2) increases to 4.

### 2. Actual elementary codes: label sharing is explicit

The codebook, family, dimension and coordinate order are fixed before sampling S. No spectral-law-dependent selection is transmitted. All codewords for a fixed a have known parsing rules.

* Repeated-label complete code: first transmit the a-bit selector-subset mask T, then transmit the a-bit address u once for each coordinate in S, in selector order followed by the data coordinate. The decoder knows s=1+|T| after the mask and checks the labels agree. Its length is a+a*s. The code is prefix-free: different masks have different fixed-length prefixes, and for a fixed mask the remaining length is fixed. The exact expected cost is a(2+a/2), so cost/I = a + 2a/(a+2) = a+2+o(1). Its repeated-label component alone is a*I, with ratio a. Omitting the mask describes only a label charge, not a complete code.
* Complete-set one-root code: transmit u once in a bits and then the a-bit mask T. Length is exactly 2a; its ratio is 4a/(a+2), tending to 4. The uniform reference Q(T union {y_u})=1/N^2 has total mass one. This code achieves H exactly. For a=0 the empty word decodes the unique set {y_0}.
* Posterior version of this elementary code: the latent witness is (u,T). Its posterior given S is a point mass, so H(witness|S)=0. The variational expression E[-log q(witness)]-H(posterior) equals 2a exactly. There is no extra label credit: S itself identifies u.

These definitions show the quadratic-to-linear repair without claiming that an inefficient code is necessary.

### 3. The source's coordinatewise fractional pressure

The canonical DNF has h_b=[x=b and y_b=1], pi_b=1/(2N), and R_b=[a] union {y_b}. For a>=1 the selector incidence constraints are sum_b lambda_b<=1. At a selector j, the unique true endpoint of a pivotal edge forces the witness gate; symmetry gives q_{j,b}=1/N. At data coordinate y_b, its witness is deterministic. In the source's outerfractional bound the nonnegative pressure is

    P(lambda) = (48/5) sum_j Inf_j log2(sum_b sqrt(q_{j,b}) lambda_b^{-11/48}).

Convexity of t^{-11/48} minimizes every selector sum at lambda_b=1/N, and convexity of -log minimizes the sum of data costs at the same weights. Saturation is optimal because increasing a weight decreases these costs. Hence

    P_min=(7/2)a^2+(11/5)a,
    P_min/I=7a-48/5+96/[5(a+2)] ~ 7a.

For a=0 there is one data gate, lambda_0=1, and pressure zero, agreeing with this polynomial. P_min is a term of a complete upper bound, not a Kraft-normalized code length by itself. The full bound (41+P_min/I)*I has the same divergent leading ratio 7a. An additional conditioned log Z term is only linear in a and does not remove this quadratic growth.

### 4. Exact single-run queue cost and its conditioned posterior

Use one common regular-gate order: selectors first, data last. Put d=a+1, gamma=6/5, theta_s(r)=min(1,(s/r)^gamma),

    E_{d,s}=e_s(theta_s(1),...,theta_s(d)),
    W_s(B)=product_{r in B} theta_s(r),  B=T union {d},  s=|B|.

Let L_a=E_T[log2(s(s+1))+log2(E_{d,s})-log2(W_s(B))]. This is a finite exact real expression (algebraic weights and logarithms), not a floating-point identity.

The one-root run chooses only gate u, its entire set B, and no children. With the threshold paper's scalar lambda=1, let Lambda_0=N log2(1+1/(2N)). The actual probability of this run is

    q_0 = 2^{-Lambda_0}/(2N) * W_s(B)/[2 s(s+1) E_{d,s}].

Thus its complete expected negative logarithm is

    C_queue=Lambda_0+a+2+L_a.

For the conditioned forest generator, fix incidence weights lambda_b=1/N and put

    Lambda_f=N log2(1+1/(2N^2)),
    Z_f=1-(1+1/(2N^2))^{-N}.

Its same one-root run, after nonempty-root conditioning, has probability

    q_* = 2^{-Lambda_f}/Z_f * 1/(2N^2) * W_s(B)/[2s(s+1) E_{d,s}],

so

    C_posterior,one-root=Lambda_f+log2 Z_f+2a+2+L_a.

The posterior here is over the specified family of one-root partition runs; exactly one such run outputs each spectral S, so its entropy subtraction is zero. At a=0, L_0=1, C_queue=log2 12 and C_posterior,one-root=2. These positive overheads belong to the queue generator, whereas the optimal elementary code has length zero.

This does NOT claim that the posterior over ALL partition forests is a point mass. When T is nonempty, selector coordinates may be assigned to other gates and produce additional distinct multi-gate partition runs. Optimizing that larger family may improve the reference. The exact finite variational formula is -log2(sum_{r in chosen family} q_*(r)); counting abstract descriptions of the same actual run several times is invalid. No all-partition optimum is claimed here.

### 5. Exact asymptotic constant for these literal queue costs

Define w(t)=1 for 0<=t<=1/2, and w(t)=(2t)^{-gamma} for 1/2<t<=1. There is a unique z>0 satisfying

    integral_0^1 z*w(t)/(1+z*w(t)) dt = 1/2.

Set

    G=integral_0^1 log2(1+z*w(t)) dt - (1/2)log2 z
      - (1/2)integral_0^1 log2 w(t) dt.

Then L_a/a -> G, and both C_queue/I and C_posterior,one-root/I tend to 2+2G. In particular,

    4 <= 2+2G <= 4+gamma*(1-1/(2 ln 2)).

This is an exact integral-defined constant, between 4 and approximately 4.3344; the decimal is illustrative.

Proof of the coefficient limit: on any sequence s/d -> 1/2, choose z_d so sum_r z_d theta_s(r)/(1+z_d theta_s(r))=s. The weights stay in a fixed compact subinterval of (0,1], so z_d stays bounded above and away from zero. Riemann sums converge, giving z_d -> z and

    log2 E_{d,s} = sum_r log2(1+z_d theta_s(r))-s log2 z_d + log2 Pr(K=s),

where K is the sum of independent Bernoulli variables with odds z_d theta_s(r) and mean s. The last term is o(d): Chebyshev gives an atom of probability at least c/sqrt(d) within O(sqrt(d)) of s; adjacent atom ratios are bounded above and below by constants in that central window, because

    min(odds)*(d-k)/(k+1) <= Pr(K=k+1)/Pr(K=k)
                                    <= max(odds)*(d-k)/(k+1).

This inequality follows by counting each (k+1)-subset k+1 times in elementary symmetric sums. Moving from that atom to s costs O(sqrt(d)) bits. This proves the coefficient limit without assuming a local central limit theorem.

Under the uniform selector-subset law, s/d ->1/2 exponentially in probability. Replacing theta_s by theta_{floor(d/2)} changes each log weight by at most gamma*|log2(s/floor(d/2))|, uniformly in r. Thus the expected selected log-weight sum, divided by d, tends to (1/2)integral log2 w; the always-present data coordinate contributes only O(1). The exceptional binomial tails contribute o(d), since every relevant cost is O(d log d). The size-choice logarithm is O(log d). Therefore L_a/a -> G. Finally Lambda_0=O(1), Lambda_f=o(1), and log2 Z_f=-a-1+o(1), proving the stated limits.

For the constant bounds, the uniform distribution on (s-1)-subsets of the a selectors has entropy log2 binom(a,s-1), and Gibbs applied to the subprobability reference B -> W_s(B)/E_{d,s} gives average subset cost at least that entropy. Averaging over s gives a-H(Bin(a,1/2)), hence G>=1. Conversely E_{d,s}<=binom(d,s) since theta<=1, and the preceding selected-weight calculation gives G<=1-(1/2)integral log2 w. Direct integration gives integral log2 w=-gamma*(1-1/(2ln2)).

### 6. The coarse conditioned-posterior theorem cost

For the same one-root assignment, M=1 and s=kappa, so the order-multiplicity and allocation-congestion terms vanish. Write

    R_a=(1/2) sum_{r=1}^a E_{K~Bin(r-1,1/2)} log2(r/(K+1))
           + E_{K~Bin(a,1/2)} log2((a+1)/(K+1)).

The forestcost expression is exactly E B_lambda = 2a+1+gamma R_a. The posterior entropy remains zero. Its residual and full theorem upper bound are

    D_a=Lambda_f+log2 Z_f+2a+1+gamma R_a,
    U_a=K_ref I+D_a,  K_ref=3+2log2 3+log2(6e).

The identity E[1/(1+Bin(r-1,1/2))]=2(1-2^{-r})/r and Jensen give R_a<=I. A binomial concentration argument gives E log2(r/(K+1))->1 (the exponentially small lower tail, with maximum log2 r, is negligible). Cesaro averaging then gives R_a/I->1. Therefore

    D_a/I -> 16/5,
    U_a/I -> K_ref+16/5.

At a=0, R_0=0 and Lambda_f+log2 Z_f=-1, so D_0=0. U_a is a coarsened theorem RHS; it is neither the literal queue cross-entropy nor the entropy of the spectral law. Discarding its K_ref I term would confuse the residual with the complete cost.

The threshold paper's separate complete one-root upper envelope is

    V_a=Lambda_0+a+2+E log2(s(s+1))
             +log2(6e) I+gamma E[s log2(d/s)],

and V_a/I -> log2(6e)+16/5. The source's finite H<10 I estimate uses further uniform inequalities and should not be misreported as an exact asymptotic queue cost.

### 7. Paid prior selection

No scale prior is needed for the fixed references above: they may be assigned weight one. If one instead uses the supplement's dyadic prior w_j=2/[3(|j|+1)(|j|+2)], the charge log2(1/w_j) must be added. For scalar lambda=1, j=0 costs log2 3, whose ratio to I vanishes. A global scalar lambda=1/N corresponds to j=-a and costs log2(3/2)+log2(a+1)+log2(a+2), also o(I). This latter observation applies to the scalar family; vector incidence weights cannot silently be substituted into a different reference family.

There is no unsupported line in the stated addressing derivation. The first unsupported universal extension would be: for every Boolean function, an admissible complete posterior reference of expected cost O(I) exists. Addressing supplies no proof of that extension.

## executed validation with domain, arithmetic, commands and outputs

# A016 stage 2: complete codes on tensor bent splices

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Follow B04 STREAM_NOTES.md v2, 2026-10-11 23:25 UTC: evaluate a-fold tensor bent splices with even core dimension m, compare an actual whole-set code and the one-root reference with selector entropy increment Delta and total influence I, and give certified cases m=2,4,6. All inputs are independent fair binary variables, outputs are +/-1, and costs and entropies are in bits. The all-parameter statements below hold for a>=1 and even m>=2; the queue limiting constant is stated for each FIXED a as m tends to infinity through even integers. Finite validation uses a=1,2. This is a lead-directed mechanism calibration and source-bound stopping-tree guard; it is not a universal FEI claim.

Result: the n=a(m+1) bit whole-set mask costs at most 3I, and the literal one-root reference costs less than 9I, uniformly in both parameters. Their cost/Delta ratios diverge exponentially with m even though their cost/I ratios remain bounded. The actual raw selector-first derivative charge has an additional tensor-spectator term and diverges relative to I when a grows at fixed m. Removing independent spectator factors repairs that particular overcharge.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-11 23:19 UTC, the complete B04 lead notes v2, and own STATUS.md before choosing this stage. Re-read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json (fei-execution-protocol/2, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2).
