# A019 stage 3: folded separator loss and exclusive private-block boundary accounts

Run 20261013T235305Z-fleet05-A019-orthogonality-extensions. Stream B04, packet A019. Full FEI remains OPEN. This stage completes the interrupted stage-2 artifacts, consumes A018's new shared-separator interface, and proves an exact ownership correction valid for arbitrary Boolean modules. Independent review of the new correction is pending.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Let r>=1 and f_j(z,y_j) be arbitrary Boolean signs. The physical separator z is one common uniform bit; private cubes y_j are mutually disjoint and independent of z. Write g_(j,b)=f_j(b,.), J_j=(I(g_(j,0))+I(g_(j,1)))/2, and d_j=Pr[g_(j,0)!=g_(j,1)]. No balance, symmetry, dimension, or positive-disagreement assumption is made. All entropy/cross-entropy units are bits. I(D) for a real derivative is its squared-Fourier Dirichlet energy.

Multiply module j by the separator character if d_j>1/2. This leaves its Fourier entropy and private influence unchanged and folds its separator influence to delta_j=min(d_j,1-d_j). Let the resulting children be a_j,b_j, q_j=E[a_j b_j]=1-2delta_j>=0, and kappa_j=I((a_j-b_j)/2). Define

Q_(-j)=product_(k!=j)q_k, delta_*=(1-product_j q_j)/2,
J=sum_j J_j, L_fold=sum_j delta_j-delta_*,
t_j=(1-Q_(-j))J_j/2+Q_(-j)kappa_j.

For the folded shared product F'=product_j f'_j, the exact influence identities are I_private(F')=J and Inf_z(F')=delta_*. The original product F differs only by an overall separator character, so H(F')=H(F), private influences are identical, and Inf_z(F)>=delta_*.

The new ownership statements are

0<=L_fold<=J-delta_*,
I(D_z F')=sum_j t_j, with 0<=t_j<=J_j.

The coefficient 1 in the private-budget loss bound is optimal: modules with g_(j,0)=1, g_(j,1)=(-1)^(y_j) give J=r/2, delta_*=1/2 and L_fold=(r-1)/2=J-delta_*.

Given finite normalized complete references for the two actual terminal products, with costs c_0,c_1, the compatible conditional-selector lift gives one normalized root reference with

CE_root <=(c_0+c_1)/2+(3/ln2)delta_*+2sum_j t_j.

In particular an exclusive module allocation

(c_(j,0)+c_(j,1))/2+2t_j<=K J_j for every j, K>=3/ln2,

is sufficient for CE_root<=K I(F). Both terminal reference bills are retained. This is a sufficient owned-budget certificate, not a universal assertion that its hypotheses hold. The paired-row entropy-subadditivity inequality posed by A018 remains open.

### 2. Character folding preserves the relevant entropy and reference costs

Multiplying f_j by chi_{z} shifts every Fourier set by symmetric difference with {z}; it is a permutation of squared Fourier probabilities. H is unchanged; pushing a complete reference through the same permutation preserves normalization, KL and CE. The physical influence of z changes from d_j to1-d_j, while all private influences stay fixed. For d_j>1/2 choose this character multiplication. The transformed correlation q_j=1-2delta_j is nonnegative.

The product acquires at most one global separator character. Its actual separator influence is delta_* or1-delta_*, and therefore at least delta_*; its entropy and private resources do not change. The exact product formula for delta_* follows from independence of the private disagreements conditioned on the common z. This is the same physical normalization as A018, now in an entropy-invariant folded orientation.

Folding is essential. The uncorrected raw loss sum_j d_j-d_* cannot be bounded by a constant times J: take every f_j=chi_z and even r, with arbitrary irrelevant private cubes. The product is constant, J=0, and raw loss r. Folding removes these character-only influences at no entropy cost. This witness is elementary; it is not an FEI counterexample.

### 3. The folded owner loss is paid by the private budget

For the private Boolean sign k_j=a_j b_j, its variance is1-q_j^2=4delta_j(1-delta_j). Poincare follows directly from Parseval, Var(k_j)<=I(k_j). On every private edge, a product changes only if at least one factor changes, so

I(k_j)<=I(a_j)+I(b_j)=2J_j.

Thus J_j>=2delta_j(1-delta_j)>=delta_j, because0<=delta_j<=1/2. Summing gives sum delta_j<=J. The shared output is pivotal exactly when an odd number of the independent folded module disagreements occur, so

L_fold=E[number of disagreements-(number mod2)]>=0.

Subtracting delta_* from sum delta_j<=J proves L_fold<=J-delta_*. This pays a duplicated separator bill using named disjoint private blocks, with no core reference or entropy assumed cheap. It does not authorize spending the same J_j again without increasing the total assigned rate.

Sharpness: if a_j=1 and b_j is its own private coordinate character, q_j=0, J_j=delta_j=1/2. Then delta_*=1/2 and L_fold=J-delta_* for every r. Also L_fold/J=1-1/r tends to1. These are actual Boolean modules in arbitrarily large dimensions, not abstract bias laws. A lower universal coefficient on J is impossible.

### 4. Exact derivative energy and one account per private block

Let A=product_j a_j, B=product_j b_j, D=(A-B)/2. For a private derivative in block j, the cross term factorizes over all other disjoint blocks. Put C_j=sum_U |U| ahat_j(U)bhat_j(U). Direct expansion yields

I(D)=(1/4)sum_j[I(a_j)+I(b_j)-2C_j Q_(-j)].

Since kappa_j=(I(a_j)+I(b_j)-2C_j)/4 and J_j=(I(a_j)+I(b_j))/2, rearranging proves

I(D)=sum_j[(1-Q_(-j))J_j/2+Q_(-j)kappa_j]=sum_j t_j.

Parallelogram/Cauchy-Schwarz gives kappa_j<=J_j, and0<=Q_(-j)<=1. Thus t_j<=((1+Q_(-j))/2)J_j<=J_j. Each t_j is assigned solely to physical private block j. The first component is the spectator contribution caused by other modules' possible mismatch; the second is the intrinsic module derivative energy. Their mixture, rather than their separately repeated worst-case bounds, is the budget that may be spent.

Apply the accepted B04-NODE-BOUNDARY@1 to the folded Boolean product:

H(F)-[H(A)+H(B)]/2 <=(3/ln2)delta_*+2sum_j t_j.

This controls one named separator step with actual terminal entropy. It is neither a universal fixed-price selector-only payment nor a proof that a cumulative adaptive boundary bill is universally bounded. Exact B may still be needed to choose or reject the separator, and no terminal budget is free.

### 5. Complete root reference with terminal CE retained

Supply finite-cost normalized complete references Q_0,Q_1 on the same private Fourier-set space for A,B. Let q=(Q_0+Q_1)/2. Let P_0,P_1 be the two true squared spectral laws and R=(P_0+P_1)/2. Fix the actual parent conditional selector law nu(.|T) on positive true rows, and an arbitrary fixed conditional on zero rows. Then

Q_root(S_z,T)=nu(S_z|T)q(T)

is one normalized reference. KL convexity/log-sum gives

KL(P_F'||Q_root)=KL(R||q)<=[KL(P_0||Q_0)+KL(P_1||Q_1)]/2.

Subtracting the actual terminal entropies therefore proves

CE_root <=(c_0+c_1)/2+Delta_z H
<=(c_0+c_1)/2+(3/ln2)delta_*+2sum_j t_j.

For disjoint terminal products, Q_b=product_j Q_(j,b) and c_b=sum_j c_(j,b); these are supplied complete references, not universally constructed ones. If each private account satisfies c_(j,avg)+2t_j<=KJ_j and K>=3/ln2, adding all accounts and the one physical separator account gives CE_root<=K[J+delta_*]<=K I(F). The root reference's character permutation restores the original un-folded product at identical CE.

This explicitly distinguishes a valid larger-rate sufficient allocation from preserving the old module rate for free. A crude terminal certificate c_(j,avg)<=C J_j yields rate max(C+2,3/ln2) at this one step. Iterating this crude rate adds slack costs; it is not the universal bridge. The exact t_j is retained to test whether existing terminal slack pays the insertion at a fixed campaign-wide K without reuse.

# A019 stage 4: arbitrary-core edits and the exact internal-pivotal correction

Full FEI remains OPEN. The half-private derivative account used by A016 extends from bent point edits to point edits of every Boolean core, and to every edit region with no internal core pivotal edges. A two-bit Boolean witness rules out removing that condition. A018's new actual Boolean entropy-excess certificate is consumed and independently verified at its eleven-bit scope; the old optional paired-row inequality is retired.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Private and shared physical bits are independent and uniform; outputs are signs. Entropy and complete reference cross-entropy (CE) are in bits. For a Boolean core g on an arbitrary finite private cube and any edit region E, set a=g, b=g(-1)^1_E, D=(a-b)/2=g1_E, J=(I(a)+I(b))/2, and kappa=I(D), with real Dirichlet energy for D. Define P_in and P_out as the contributions to I(g) of directed pivotal edges with both endpoints in E and both endpoints outside E, respectively, using the same normalization as total influence. Then exactly

2 kappa - J = P_in - P_out.

Consequently kappa<=J/2 if and only if P_in<=P_out. This includes arbitrary cores and independent edit sets (no two vertices of E are adjacent), not only flat-spectrum cores. For a point edit in dimension m, delta=2^-m, kappa=m delta/2 and J>=m delta. There is no neighboring-disagreement lower bound.

For any number of disjoint arbitrary private cores g_j, two shared bits z,w, and arbitrary Boolean shared masks A_j(z,w), form F=product_j g_j(y_j)(-1)^(A_j(z,w)1_Ej(y_j)). Assume each E_j has density<=1/2 and P_in,j<=P_out,j. With J=I_private(F), d=Inf_z(F)+Inf_w(F), and E_zw the squared spectral mass containing both shared bits, the z-then-w stopping prefix has exact derivative bill T=T_private+2E_zw, T_private<=2J, and T<=2J+d<=2I(F). Filtering any exact bad-node threshold decreases T. All terminal reference costs are retained: if four supplied normalized complete terminal references have average cost cbar, a normalized root reference exists with CE<=cbar+T+(3/ln2)d. In particular cbar<=C J implies CE<=max(C+2,1+3/ln2)I(F). This is a conditional certificate transfer, not an assertion that arbitrary cores have such references.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json completely, revision2026-10-11-execution-v2; BOARD.md snapshot2026-10-14 02:43 UTC; B04 STREAM_NOTES.md v2; own STATUS.md, LEARNED.md, and previous stage REPORT.md and checker/finalizer. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Edge partition proves the exact correction

For an oriented private edge (x,x^i), a real function h contributes (h(x)-h(x^i))^2/(4*2^m) to I(h). Partition those edges into inside E, outside E, and crossing its boundary. Inside E, both a and b have the same pivotal status as g, while D=g; thus their contributions to J and kappa agree. Outside E, J receives the contribution of g and D is zero. Across the boundary, one child has the original two signs and the other reverses one endpoint. Exactly one child is pivotal, so the contribution to J is 1/(2*2^m), while D has one sign and one zero, contributing 1/(4*2^m). Hence crossing terms cancel in 2kappa-J, inside terms contribute P_in, and outside terms contribute -P_out. This proves the identity and its iff condition with no Fourier regularity assumption.

An independent edit set has P_in=0, so every core satisfies half-private. The stronger structural condition is only absence of internal CORE pivotal edges: adjacent vertices inside E are allowed if g agrees along them. For a point edit, let r be the number of original pivotal directions incident to that point. Each original edge contributes 2delta to I(g), so I(g)>=2r delta. Reversing the point changes private influence by 2delta(m-2r), giving J=I(g)+delta(m-2r)>=m delta. The signed point indicator has kappa=m delta/2, agreeing with the general partition formula. This closes A016's half-private arbitrary-core subquestion without requiring a new class census.

### 2. Sharp obstruction to arbitrary edit regions

On two private bits, indexed x1 as the low bit, take g=[1,1,-1,1] and E={0,2}, i.e. x1=0. Then b=[-1,1,1,1], I(g)=I(b)=1, J=1, and D=[1,0,-1,0] has kappa=3/4. Its internal pivotal contribution is1/2 and external pivotal contribution is0; thus 2kappa-J=1/2. The child disagreement is1/2 and child correlation is0, so separator-character folding does not repair this failure. The false statement 'all arbitrary certified-core edits have kappa<=J/2' is refuted already on an actual two-bit core. Nothing here is an FEI counterexample.

### 3. Product ownership, including the signed term

Fix one shared edge, holding the other shared bit constant, and let a_j,b_j be its module endpoint functions. Write J_j,edge=(I(a_j)+I(b_j))/2, kappa_j=I((a_j-b_j)/2), q_j=E[a_j b_j], Q_-j=product_(h!=j)q_h. The edge changes either no private region or E_j. In the changed case q_j=1-2density(E_j)>=0; in the unchanged case q_j=1. The hypotheses ensure kappa_j<=J_j,edge/2 in both cases.

A private Fourier cross-energy expansion gives C_j=sum_U |U| ahat_j(U)bhat_j(U), kappa_j=(J_j,edge-C_j)/2, and

I(D_product)=sum_j t_j,  t_j=(1-Q_-j)J_j,edge/2+Q_-j kappa_j.

Indeed the two product endpoint energies are additive over disjoint blocks, while their cross-energy is sum_j C_j Q_-j. This rederives the prior ownership formula without an entropy claim. Since Q_-j is in[0,1], t_j<=J_j,edge/2. More precisely define R_j,edge=P_in,j,edge-P_out,j,edge for the effective edit region (empty when unchanged). Then

t_j=J_j,edge/2+Q_-j R_j,edge/2.

Thus the term controlling an arbitrary-region extension is signed and exactly localized to an owned private block. It is not the total private influence charged again.

Average the two possible edges for each shared direction and add both directions. Let u_j=sum_direction E_other t_j,edge. The average J_j,edge in either direction is the same physical private J_j. Therefore u_j<=J_j, with one total account per module, and

T_private=2sum_j u_j = 2J+W,
W=sum_j sum_direction E_other[Q_-j R_j,edge].

Under the structural hypothesis W<=0. Parseval proves T_private=2sum_S |S_private| |S intersect {z,w}| Fhat(S)^2. The root z derivative also charges the remaining w coordinate, exactly2E_zw. The child w derivatives have no remaining shared coordinate. Thus T=T_private+2E_zw. Since E_zw<=min(Inf_z,Inf_w), 2E_zw<=d, proving T<=2J+d. This is uniform in module count, private dimension and small disagreement. For arbitrary regions of density<=1/2, the exact formula with W remains true even when W is positive; the two-bit witness shows why dropping W is unsupported.

### 4. Complete terminal reference transfer and its limitation

Let P_b be the actual private product spectrum at shared leaf b in{0,1}^2, with supplied normalized finite-cost reference Q_b, and cost c_b. Set R=average_b P_b and Qbar=average_b Q_b. Define Q(L,U)=nu_actual(L|U) Qbar(U), where nu_actual is the parent's conditional shared Fourier law on a private row; on a zero true row choose any fixed normalized conditional. This is a finite normalized reference, with support wherever the true law is positive. Joint-to-marginal log-sum gives KL(R||Qbar)<=average_b KL(P_b||Q_b). Consequently

CE_Q(F)<=cbar+H(F)-average_b H(F_b).

Telescoping the reviewed B04 node bound on the prescribed physical tree gives the entropy difference <=T+(3/ln2)d. Both terminal CE costs and the remaining shared mixed energy are included. Inserting T<=2J+d gives the conditional rate stated above. No product terminal-reference assumption is needed: arbitrary supplied complete references for the four actual products are allowed.

For a proposed rate K, the exact signed sufficient ledger is

cbar+2J+W+2E_zw+(3/ln2)d <= K(J+d).

This formula exposes the next gap. Half-private ownership by itself does NOT show that supplied terminals leave enough rate4 slack: the term (1+3/ln2)d may require a positive unused core reserve, unlike A016's bent point-edit reserve. The first unsupported universal line is existence of complete terminal references and an admissible separator policy whose SAME remaining slack pays that signed inequality through repeated prefixes. Repeating the conditional C+2 overhead at every level is not a universal proof. This stage does not claim the rate4 bent-spine theorem for arbitrary cores. The new A018 point-product witness moreover forbids replacing this debt by a universal loss-only entropy price.

### 5. Source-bound independent check of the new Boolean obstruction

Independently construct the actual eleven-bit parent and both six-bit modules from the point-defect truth-table rule. Recursive Walsh transforms and rational prime-log bookkeeping reproduce

131072 ln(2)[H(parent)-2H(module)] = 491071 ln31 -231361 ln481 -27900 ln15 -262144 ln2.

Pure integer arithmetic verifies 31^491071 > 481^231361*15^27900*2^262144. Hence the positive entropy excess is independently confirmed. This review covers the finite Boolean falsifier only; it does not certify A018's entire asymptotic or reference theorem. The earlier zero-mean scalar candidate in A019 stage3 is now explicitly retired. None of the proved ownership identities depends on it.

# A019 stage5: private-query empty-row transport, sharp curvature and zero-row repair

Full FEI remains OPEN. This stage consumes A018/A020's independent full-scope acceptance of A019's previous theorem and A020's warning that positive raw W is not necessarily a localized debt. It closes an explicit low-disagreement reference-transport case: after a genuine private-core query with two balanced children, an absent-frequency convention on zero true rows makes the net activation-reference surcharge nonpositive, with arbitrary supplied complete child references retained. Without that convention an essential sparse query has fixed positive surcharge as disagreement tends to zero. A sharp bound also prices the scalar empty-row curvature when an inherited core and a nonlinear two-bit mask both have nonzero means. The remaining general signed refund is stated, not discarded.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

All physical bits are independent and uniform, outputs Boolean signs, and entropy, KL and complete reference CE use bits. Let s be a nonlinear Boolean sign mask on two shared bits, so sigma=E s is plus or minus1/2. Let h(q,u) be any Boolean private core, with actual children h_0,h_1, means beta_0,beta_1, mean beta=(beta_0+beta_1)/2 and physical query disagreement d=Pr[h_0!=h_1]. Set F=1 at gate x0 and h*s at x1; both child fallbacks h_b*s stay nonconstant when h_b is balanced. The same supplied finite normalized mask reference Q_s and supplied normalized finite-cost core references are carried throughout.

Define eta(t)=-t log2(t), Psi(a)=eta((1+a)^2/4)+eta((1-a)^2/4)+1-a^2-eta(a^2)/2, and A=(9/8)log2(3)-1. Then the actual private-query profile curvature is sharply bounded by

0 <= Psi(beta*sigma)-[Psi(beta_0*sigma)+Psi(beta_1*sigma)]/2 <= A*d.

For the physical-label mixture reference introduced by A018, let c_v be its supplied core cost, c_s the fixed mask cost, and D_v its exact nonnegative posterior-label KL refund at a core-tree node v. The full cost is CE_v=c_v/2+c_s/2+Psi(beta_v*sigma)-D_v. If the parent core reference is the actual queried-frequency conditional lift of the average child references, define D_core=average_child KL_core-KL_parent_core>=0. The exact net reference insertion is

CE_parent-average_child CE = Delta H_core/2 + curvature + Lambda,
Lambda=average_child D_child-D_parent-D_core/2.

If beta_0=beta_1=0 and the parent reference conditional on every zero true data row assigns the queried frequency absent, then Lambda<=0. Hence CE_parent-average_child CE<=Delta H_core/2, uniformly as d tends to zero. Supplied terminal costs are not removed. The equal-split convention on zero rows does NOT satisfy any universal Lambda<=K*d: an actual rate4-certified core family below has Lambda=(1/2)log2(18/17)>0 and d=2^-k for every k>=1. This is a no-go for an explicit reference transport rule, not FEI.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Reconstruct A018's exact physical-label reference at the needed scope

For G=h*s, disjoint physical blocks give mean mu=beta*sigma, spectrum P_G=P_h tensor P_s, and supplied reference Q_G=Q_h tensor Q_s, cost c_h+c_s. With F=1 at x0 and G at x1, its nonempty G rows have two x coefficients Ghat(T)/2; the empty row has coefficients(1+mu)/2 and(1-mu)/2. Thus H(F)=H(G)/2+Psi(mu), I(F)=I(G)/2+(1-mu)/2. The data law is R=(P_G+delta_empty)/2. Use the SAME physical mixture q=(Q_G+delta_empty)/2 and lift it by F's actual conditional x-frequency law, including an arbitrary normalized conditional on zero true rows. This is a normalized finite-cost complete reference.

The tagged physical-branch KL is KL(P_G||Q_G)/2. Marginalizing its tag yields KL(R||q), and their difference is posterior-tag KL D>=0. Only the empty G row has tag ambiguity, so exactly

D=(1+mu^2)/2 KL_Ber(mu^2/(1+mu^2) || Q_h(empty)Q_s(empty)/(1+Q_h(empty)Q_s(empty))).

Consequently CE_F=(c_h+c_s)/2+Psi(mu)-D. Finite CE excludes unsupported positive true atoms; if mu=0, the binary formula simply becomes D=(1/2)log2(1+Q_h(empty)Q_s(empty)). This independently reconstructs A018's normalized reference identity and posterior-refund clause, with no author checker imported. Review scope does not include A060's sharp Gamma optimization or A018's entire negative-prior-change claim.

### 2. Sharp biased empty-row curvature after an actual core exposure

Set phi(b)=Psi(b/2). Evenness of Psi makes the sign of sigma immaterial. For b!=0,

phi''(b)=(1/4)log2[b^2/(4-b^2)].

Thus K(b)=-phi''(b) is nonnegative, even, and decreasing in |b| on[-1,1], with an integrable logarithmic singularity at zero. Extend K by zero outside that interval. For child-mean midpoint t and radius r=|beta_0-beta_1|/2, the Jensen gap is exactly

(1/2) integral_-r^r (r-|u|) K(t+u) du.

This is maximized at t=0. To verify rather than assume rearrangement, write the triangular weight as an integral of centered interval indicators. An even decreasing nonnegative function integrates most over a centered interval of prescribed length: moving such an interval away from zero replaces its nearer endpoint segment by a farther segment. Integrate that inequality over the layer lengths. Truncation and monotone convergence handle the integrable singularity. The derivative is absolutely continuous, so the displayed second-derivative integral identity remains valid across zero.

The gap is therefore <=phi(0)-phi(r). That function of r is convex, vanishes at0, and lies below its endpoint chord r[phi(0)-phi(1)]. Exactly Psi(0)=2, Psi(1/2)=3-(9/8)log2(3), giving A=phi(0)-phi(1). Actual Boolean child disagreement satisfies |beta_0-beta_1|<=2d by averaging pointwise differences. Hence curvature<=A*d. Positivity is concavity. A is positive and below1, as 2^8<3^9<2^16.

The bound is sharp: h is the actual queried character, with means1 and-1 and d=1. Both children can instead remain nonconstant by taking h_0 a rare point-defect sign and h_1=-h_0: their means tend to plus/minus1 while d stays1 and the ratio tends to A. This is an all-dimensional proof; finite tests are not a continuum bridge. In physical F-query units the scalar charge is 2A*Inf_q(F), since Inf_q(F)=d/2.

### 3. Exact signed insertion with one carried core hierarchy

Let P_b be the two child core spectra on unqueried rows, and Q_b their supplied complete references. On the parent core's positive unqueried row U use its actual conditional queried-frequency law nu_h(L|U). Set Q_h(L,U)=nu_h(L|U)(Q_0(U)+Q_1(U))/2; on zero true rows choose a specified normalized conditional. Marginal log-sum gives

KL(P_h||Q_h)<=average_b KL(P_b||Q_b),
D_core=average_b KL(P_b||Q_b)-KL(P_h||Q_h)>=0,
c_h=average_b c_b+Delta H_core-D_core.

Substitute this identity into the three exact activation CE identities from section1. It gives the stated CE insertion with Lambda=average D_b-D_parent-D_core/2. Every supplied child CE, the fixed mask reference and the posterior refunds remain. This is the precise correction to treating the A018 refund as hereditary available credit. No separate bound on raw W is required. The reference tree carries its existing sources; it is not reset to unknown exact terminal spectra. Consistency of the incoming parent core reference with the displayed child lift is an explicit hypothesis, not a license to replace an arbitrary previously fixed incoming reference after a query.

For any finite core-only stopping tree, the profile curvatures telescope as phi(beta_root)-sum_leaf pi phi(beta_leaf). Their local bounds sum to <=A sum_internal pi d_v=A[I(h_root)-sum_leaf pi I(h_leaf)]. The D and D_core terms must also telescope as signed potentials; a nonnegative D at each individual node does NOT permit deleting the positive average child D in the parent insertion. The bound is a single physical reserve for the profile term, not a new allowance per depth.

### 4. Uniform zero-row repair when both children remain balanced

Suppose beta_0=beta_1=0. Then the entire parent core empty unqueried row has zero true mass, not merely one Fourier coefficient. Specify nu_h on zero true rows by putting all its mass on the queried frequency ABSENT, as a fixed convention in the incoming reference construction before sampling. This gives Q_h(empty)=[Q_0(empty)+Q_1(empty)]/2. This allowed convention preserves normalization and every positive true core cost; changing it may nonetheless affect activation CE because the constant physical branch puts true mass on the empty row.

With t=Q_s(empty) fixed, all three activation refunds reduce to D_v=(1/2)log2(1+t Q_v(empty)). Concavity of log(1+t a) yields average D_b<=D_parent. Therefore Lambda<=-D_core/2<=0. The profile curvature vanishes, and CE_parent-average CE_child<=Delta H_core/2, as claimed. This holds for arbitrary finite-cost supplied child references, arbitrary nonlinear mask reference, every private dimension and arbitrary small query disagreement. The fallback h_b*s remains nonconstant because h_b is balanced and s is nonconstant. No entropy/I bound for h itself is supplied by this reference identity.

### 5. Essential rare-query obstruction under the equally split zero-row convention

For every k>=1, let u be one private bit and v a disjoint k-bit cube. Put h_0=chi_u and h_1=chi_u*(-1)^1_(v=0). Expose one physical query q choosing these children. Both children are balanced and nonconstant; q is essential with d=2^-k. The parent's and children's true empty core coefficients vanish. Supply Q_b=(P_b+delta_empty)/2. Each child core KL is exactly1. On every positive true parent row the conditional lift gives Q_h=P_h/2. The empty unqueried row has reference mass1/2 and true mass0. If its conditional splits equally between queried frequency absent/present, then Q_h(empty)=1/4. Parent core KL is also1, so D_core=0.

Take s a positive-mean two-bit point defect with its exact flat squared spectrum Q_s, Q_s(empty)=1/4 and c_s=2. The child activation refunds are (1/2)log2(9/8), while the parent refund is (1/2)log2(17/16). Thus

Lambda=(1/2)log2(18/17)>0,
Lambda/d=2^(k-1)log2(18/17) -> infinity.

The decisive sign is the integer comparison18>17. This refutes only the stated universal query-price inequality for the equal-split reference transport, including any attempt to cancel it by D_core/2. These references are not pathological unsupported costs: every parent and child core reference is rate4 eligible. A character contributes I1,H0. The other phase is respectively a point defect on k or k+1 fresh bits, and the previously proved H_phase<=4I_phase gives H_core+1<=4I_core-3. Thus at least3 bits of certified core slack remain; its half-branch reserve exceeds the constant debt, which is below1/2. No FEI or owned-child-bank theorem is refuted.

Under the absent-query convention instead, Q_h(empty)=1/2, D_parent equals the child refunds, and Lambda=0 for this same essential rare-query family. The full branch references and all terminal costs are kept; only a reference-only row assignment changes. This provides an exact low-disagreement repair on an unbounded structured class, not a new positive-correlation result or new activation constant.

### 6. Changed universal gap and exact first unsupported line

The profile curvature for biased cores and nonlinear two-bit masks is now paid sharply by one query reserve. The remaining term Lambda is quantified and controlled nonpositively for two balanced child cores under a specified zero-row convention. For general children of nonzero means, the empty row has true mass and its parent conditional can no longer be chosen freely. The first unsupported universal line is a support-aware policy and unused certified child slack that pay the positive part of Lambda while retaining the same Q_s, the full c_b costs, the core-query refund and later descendants. Neither the bounded scalar curvature nor the false paired-row subadditivity supplies that bank. The successful balanced-child repair identifies the exact hypothesis to remove next; enlarging the sparse-family dimension is unnecessary.

    Delta <= (2/ln 2)s + a h2(s/a).

## Complete derivation and first unsupported line

Write a_x(T)=ghat_x(T). The signed coefficient is

    Fhat(S,T)=2^-a sum_x (-1)^(S dot x) a_x(T).

Consequently sum_S Fhat(S,T)^2=R_T=2^-a sum_x a_x(T)^2. Conditional on T, the selector spectral law is nu_T(S)=Fhat(S,T)^2/R_T. The data marginal entropy gives the exact identity

    Delta = B_W + M_W,
    B_W = sum_T R_T H(nu_T),
    M_W = H(R)-2^-a sum_x H(ghat_x^2),
    s = sum_T R_T sum_S |S| nu_T(S).

These equations retain signed collisions; replacing sums of amplitudes by sums of squared amplitudes changes the problem. The entropy bound H(nu_T)<=sum_j h2(P_nu_T[j in S]) and twice-applied concavity give B_W<=a h2(s/a). Applying the cube logarithmic Sobolev inequality, in natural units Ent(v^2)<=(1/2)sum_j E(v(x)-v(x^j))^2, to |a_x(T)|/sqrt(R_T), and summing over T, gives (ln2)M_W<=2s by data Parseval and |abs(u)-abs(v)|<=|u-v|. The two-point inequality and tensorization are supplied in the source's §2; this is a reconstruction of the existing channel estimate, not a newly opened broad candidate. Adding yields the lead's inequality. This argument does not establish sharpness of the lead's asymptotic constant, which is assigned to A018.

The new finite certificate is stronger: in all seven exhaustive domains,

    Delta <= (171/200)[(2/ln2)s + a h2(s/a)],

with strict inequality whenever s>0 and equality at s=0. There are 300 zero-selector tables and 131,320 positive-selector tables. The zero case is analytic: no selector edge changes F, so all branches agree, and Delta=0. All positive cases are certified by outward rational interval comparison, not floating-point arithmetic. Every truth table is a genuine Boolean function by construction.

The first unsupported line toward a universal constant would be a nonreusing payment of the entire rare term sum_v w_v I_sel(v) log(a_v/I_sel(v)) while retaining terminal data entropy. The finite cap does not pay this term. No universal strict ratio gap at densities bounded away from zero is proved by this stage.

## Named boundary witnesses

1. Coincident support and affine parity cells. On two control bits, take complementary affine cells A0={x0+x1=0}, A1={x0+x1=1}, with leaves y and -y. The actual F=(-1)^(x0+x1)y is a character: H=0, I=3. Dropping the cross terms in the disjoint-leaf-support mixture ledger predicts H=2 and I=2. Both cells are affine and disjoint physically; the two branch Fourier supports coincide. Giving both cells the same leaf instead produces F=y, H=0, I=1, so cancellation can also erase the selector influence entirely.

2. Unequal affine cells. Use A0={x0=0}, A1={(x0,x1)=(1,0)}, A2={(1,1)}, with leaves y,-y,y. Cell probabilities are 1/2,1/4,1/4. Then F=h(x)y, where h is a two-control one-point defect. All four Fourier masses of h are 1/4, so H(F)=2 and I(F)=2. The false mixture entropy is H(1/2,1/4,1/4)+sum_l p_l H(q_l)=3, where q_l=ahat_l^2/p_l. Its influence is 1+sum_l p_l E_q_l|S|=7/4. Thus even unequal affine cells require both interference and actual selector influence. The general nested fixtures implement this same defect at a=2 and analogous partitions at higher a.

3. Nearly identical branches with coincident full Fourier support. On four data bits let g=(-1)^(y0 y1+y2 y3) and flip only y=0 to obtain g'. They disagree on 1/16 of the cube. Their Fourier coefficients are respectively sigma_T/4 and sigma_T/4-1/8, so every data Fourier label is present in both branches. Their physical splice has selector influence 1/16 and nonzero entropy increment. Branch closeness does not imply orthogonality, disjoint spectral support or zero entropy debt. The exact signed Walsh coefficients and entropy intervals are saved in fixtures.json. The higher-selector fixtures select this point flip on a singleton cell or on a parity cell; the control influence is checked directly against physical edges.

The mandatory analytic recovered bent-splice guard is reconstructed in SELECTOR_FALSIFIER_SUITE.tex. For even m, delta=2^-m and e=sqrt(delta), the parent coefficients are e sigma_T-delta and delta in its two selector sectors, while g' has e sigma_T-2delta. Expanding -t^2 ln(t^2), with a uniformly bounded Taylor remainder for m>=4, gives Delta/delta=m+3/ln2+O(delta), so no fixed selector-only increment constant survives. The full physical influence is m/2+(m+1)delta. This confirms divergent local debt while preserving the data influence available for FEI. SELECTOR_FAILURE_SCOPE.json records the exact false identities and excluded hypotheses.

The known arbitrary-signed-leaf admission X005 remains applicable: singleton affine cells with g_x(y)=h(x)y encode arbitrary h. The family is FEI-strength after tensor amplification. This stage supplies falsifier coverage, not a new universal reduction.

Integer Walsh numerators are squared before normalization. The script checks Parseval, each partial data-row Parseval identity, physical versus spectral selector influence, and average-child versus full data influence in every case. All logarithms use exact Fraction arithmetic for

    ln t = 2 sum_(j=0)^39 z^(2j+1)/(2j+1) + remainder,
    z=(t-1)/(t+1), 1<=t<2,

Range reduction expresses ln(k) through ln(t)+e ln2. Endpoints are rounded outward at integer scale 2^100. Entropy sums and averages are rounded outward, including signed subtraction. Natural units eliminate division by ln2 from the inequality: the target is 2s+a h_nat(s/a). A positive rational lower bound on target minus Delta_nat certifies the original inequality. The cap pass checks the upper interval endpoint of Delta_nat/target_nat is less than 171/200 for every positive-selector table. Source series error and all rounding are included.

The larger 1,024 random-table sample's best moderate witness is mask 9205610344637496819, branches [50975,51227,61438,27302], a=2,m=4, s=13/16, density=13/32, with ratio about 0.6019871387555503. This is finite search evidence with a certified witness interval, not an optimality claim. direct_check.py reconstructs all 32 named fixture Fourier numerators by independent O(N^2) character sums and compares physical edge counts, using no Walsh butterfly. It is a second implementation by this agent, not an independent-agent review.

One bounded computation ran at a time. The frozen estimates are below 200 MB, so the >300 MB memory-gate condition did not apply. No dimension was raised automatically, and no GPU, window, background helper, git, external message, brain write or shared-tree mutation was used.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Exact Boolean counterexample to the proposed 81/100 cap

Let f on five bits have negative-output truth mask 474167, assignments ordered little-endian, and choose coordinate i=0. Equivalently its two four-bit branches have negative-output masks 871 and 357. They differ exactly at data vertices 1 and 9, adjacent in data coordinate 3; D=-1 at those two vertices and zero elsewhere. Thus d=2/16=1/8 and

    I(D) = 3/16,
    2I(D) = d log2(1/d) = 3/8.

The support-isoperimetric step in the lead's node bound is equality on this witness, so its excess ratio is not an artifact of a loose support-boundary estimate.

Direct character sums give the parent Fourier-mass histogram: eight atoms of mass 1/16, fourteen of mass 1/64, two of mass 9/64, and eight zeros. Each child has ten masses 1/64 and six masses 9/64. Hence

    H(f) = 5 - (9/16)log2 3,
    H(g) = H(h) = 6 - (27/16)log2 3,
    Delta_0 = (9/8)log2 3 - 1.

In natural units the denominator is (3/8)(1+ln2), and therefore

    R = Delta_0 / [(3/ln2)/8 + 3/8]
      = (9ln3-8ln2)/(3(1+ln2))
      = 0.8548839705904677... > 17/20 > 81/100.

The strict sign R>17/20 is certified by the rational logarithm intervals in direct_certificates.json (equivalently 180ln3-211ln2>51). The finite Walsh arrays and the physical derivative energy are reconstructed with direct O(N^2) character sums and exact Fraction arithmetic, without the batched census kernel. This is a second implementation by A020, not independent-agent acceptance.

The weaker pilot witness mask 775 at coordinate 1 has d=3/16, I(D)=1/4, and ratio 0.8180317633..., independently certified greater than 81/100. The full five-bit scan has 349 certified failing coordinate pairs among retained symmetry representatives; these are not an orbit classification or a count of all labeled failures. No interval comparison against 81/100 remains unresolved. Candidate storage is deliberately capped at 256 records; the integer count includes every failure.

Scope guard: exact erasure at the stronger witness is

    B_0 = (5/8)log2 5 - 1,
    B_0/d = 5log2 5 - 8 = 3.60964047... <4.

Indeed its eight nontrivial selector fibers have masses 4/64 and 1/64, giving (5/8)h2(1/5). The inequality B_0<4d follows from 5^5<2^12. Thus this node is GOOD for the lead's exact-B threshold K=4. The witness refutes an unlocalized 0.81 discount, not the K=4 bad-node-filtered stopping-tree proposal, FEI, or the original node-boundary theorem.

### 2. Exact full-domain coverage without forbidden block mixing

Write a five-bit truth table as a uint32 mask. Output complement changes no squared Fourier spectrum, physical influence, derivative energy or restriction entropy. Select the output sign with at most 16 negative entries. For every nonconstant function, translate a negative input to the origin; its mask is then odd. All 2^31 odd masks are scanned. Exactly 2^30 of them have at most 16 negative entries. The two constants are handled analytically: selector entropy increment is zero; no singleton has d>=1/8.

Within selector coordinates {0,1,2} and data coordinates {3,4}, reorder physical influences increasingly. These permutations preserve the a=3 block and its entropy increment. Do not permute a selector with a data coordinate. Input translations preserve the sorted influences. Among all odd translates, keep the numerically smallest mask. Every nonconstant orbit therefore has a retained representative. Testing all five coordinates at each representative also covers every physical singleton split, because the allowed symmetries only relabel coordinates or swap children and preserve the tested quantities. Ties in influence ordering may leave duplicates; uniqueness is neither used nor claimed.

Full run counts: 2,147,483,648 raw masks; 1,073,741,824 with minority size at most 16; 236,906,367 satisfying the within-block influence ordering; 16,973,810 translation-minimal survivors. On these survivors, 84,854,838 singleton nodes have d>=1/8. The ordered survivor stream SHA256 is fc177ad24a7e446c4885143142ef82ed032d7245f0b54fc49e2783709fdd85f0.

The certified inequalities are, on every actual five-bit singleton with d>=1/8,

    Delta_i < (9/10)[(3/ln2)d+2I(D_i)],

and on every a=3,m<=2 multiplexer with positive s=I_sel,

    Delta < (9/10)[min(3,(2/ln2)s)+3h2(s/3)].

If s=0, all branches agree and Delta=0 exactly. For m=0,1 the saved lower-domain verification separately enumerates all 256 and 65,536 tables. The m=2 certificate is not inferred from numerical extrapolation or arbitrary NPN classes. The finite 9/10 statements do not imply their universal analogues: the already established bent-splice ratio tends to one when d tends to zero.

### 3. Integer spectrum and interval-sign certification

For n=5 the FWHT returns even integer numerators W_S; write c_S=W_S/2, so p_S=c_S^2/256. The entropy numerator is sum_S G(|c_S|), where G(k)=-k^2 ln(k^2/256). G is enclosed on an integer grid of scale 2^40. Every exact physical selector edge count is compared with spectral influence, and every parent has sum c_S^2=256.

For a singleton i, the two child Fourier numerators are c_T+c_(T+i) and c_T-c_(T+i), each normalized by 16. Thus the census's signed child calculation retains interference before squaring. Define dnum=sum_(S contains i)c_S^2 and enum=sum_(S contains i)(|S|-1)c_S^2; d=dnum/256 and I(D)=enum/256. The comparison in natural units is

    Delta_nat <= 3d+2ln2 I(D).

All numerator differences use outward endpoints. The 81/100 question is decided by integer cross multiplication: certified lower Delta > (81/100) upper denominator records a failure; upper Delta < (81/100) lower denominator records a pass; anything else is counted as unresolved. The final unresolved count is zero. The 9/10 cap is checked against every positive denominator using integer cross multiplication, with zero failures.

For the selector block, data branches are formed explicitly from truth-table bits at x+8y. The eight two-bit child entropy numerators use a precomputed exact 16-table bank. For every possible snum in 0..768, a rational outward envelope is prepared for 256*[min(3ln2,2s)+3h_nat(s/3)]. The extra factor eight avoids rounding the average branch entropy. Both 9/10 and the original bound are tested on every survivor.

Logarithms use range reduction to t in [1,2) and

    ln t = 2 sum_(j=0)^39 z^(2j+1)/(2j+1) + remainder,
    z=(t-1)/(t+1),
    0<=remainder<=2z^81/[81(1-z^2)].

All series and tails are exact Fractions before outward fixed-grid rounding. Direct witness certification uses 60 terms and unrounded Fraction endpoints. The lower m=0,1 continuation reuses the prior 2^100 outward checker; its stronger precision and source hash are recorded. No floating-point value establishes a sign or the uniform 9/10 cap. Floating ratios only select discovered best witnesses, so exact symbolic maximizer classification is not claimed.

Overflow check: each nonnegative entropy contribution is at most 256/e, so a deliberately loose entropy-numerator sum bound is 8192*2^40. Parent-minus-child numerators multiplied by eight and then by 100 have absolute value below 2^63. The largest singleton denominator numerator is bounded using dnum<=256 and enum<=1024; its integer cross products are also below 2^63. The exact log-bank entries and array dtypes are int64. The actual sign checks therefore cannot wrap in the prescribed five-bit domain.

# A020 stage 3: joint shared phase defeats dimension-free repeated-owner payment

Full FEI remains OPEN. This stage independently reviews A019's single-separator ownership correction and refutes its natural extension to an unbounded shared block. The extension is a diagnostic proposed here, not a statement attributed to A019. The obstruction is connected, survives every global character orientation, and has a complete reference costing at most 4I.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

All cubes are uniform, all functions are Boolean signs, and entropy/CE are in bits. Write shared bits as Z_i in {0,1}, with sign characters (-1)^Z_i. For arbitrary integers k,r>=1, take disjoint private bits y_1,...,y_r and define A(Z)=product_i Z_i, f_j(Z,y_j)=(-1)^(A(Z)y_j), and F=product_j f_j=(-1)^(A(Z)(xor_j y_j)). Set delta=2^-k and J=I_private(F).

For a choice of shared characters chi_{C_j}, let f'_j=chi_{C_j}f_j and F'=product_j f'_j. Define the repeated shared influence loss L(C)=sum_j I_Z(f'_j)-I_Z(F'). The target tested is whether min_C L(C)<=KJ, or even <=K I(F), for a universal K independent of k,r. Both statements are false. This does not refute one-bit A019-FOLDED-SEPARATOR-OWNER@1, nor establish an FEI counterexample.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-14 00:19 UTC, B04 STREAM_NOTES.md v2 (2026-10-11 23:25 UTC), own STATUS.md and prior stage outputs. Read the execution protocol in full: 2026-10-11-execution-v2. The stream lead's finite census request was settled in stage 2; dispatch now consumes this new ownership dependency. No paired-row scalar search is repeated. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### Independent reconstruction of the one-bit owner theorem

For arbitrary modules with one common bit z and disjoint private cubes, fold each module by chi_z so its child correlation q_j=E[a_j b_j]>=0. Then delta_j=(1-q_j)/2<=1/2. Character multiplication permutes squared Fourier labels and complete reference labels, preserving H and CE; private influences are unchanged. Independence of private cubes gives delta_*=(1-product_j q_j)/2. The original product's separator influence is delta_* or 1-delta_*, hence at least delta_*.

The Boolean private function a_j b_j has variance 1-q_j^2=4delta_j(1-delta_j). Parseval/Poincare and the physical-edge union bound imply 4delta_j(1-delta_j)<=I(a_j b_j)<=I(a_j)+I(b_j)=2J_j. Thus J_j>=delta_j. The loss sum delta_j-delta_* is nonnegative because the number of disagreements minus its parity is nonnegative. It is at most J-delta_*. With a_j=1,b_j a private character, equality holds for every r, proving the optimal coefficient 1.

For completeness independently expand D=(product a_j-product b_j)/2 in physical private derivatives. With C_j=sum_U |U| ahat_j(U)bhat_j(U) and Q_-j=product_{h!=j}q_h,

I(D)=sum_j [I(a_j)+I(b_j)-2Q_-j C_j]/4
=sum_j [(1-Q_-j)J_j/2+Q_-j kappa_j],

where kappa_j=I((a_j-b_j)/2). The pointwise squared derivative inequality gives kappa_j<=J_j; each summand t_j is nonnegative and <=J_j. There is one such account per disjoint private block.

The reference part of A019's claim follows conditionally on the accepted B04-NODE-BOUNDARY@1: Delta_z H<=(3/ln2)delta_*+2sum t_j. For supplied normalized terminal references Q_0,Q_1 let q=(Q_0+Q_1)/2 and R=(P_0+P_1)/2. The parent erased law is R. Multiplying q by the actual parent conditional selector law yields a normalized root reference and KL(parent||root)=KL(R||q)<=avg KL(P_b||Q_b), by log-sum. Therefore CE_root<=avg c_b+Delta_z H. Summing the supplied exclusive allocations avg c_(j,b)+2t_j<=KJ_j gives CE_root<=K I(original) for K>=3/ln2. No existence of these allocations has been supplied. Review verdict: HOLDS at stated scope, with its previously accepted node-boundary dependency retained; the private-owner/derivative proof is independently reconstructed above and checked on 256 arbitrary ordered module pairs. No repair to the source theorem is needed.

### A connected multibit obstruction, including all character orientations

Every private coordinate of F has influence delta. A shared coordinate is pivotal precisely when the other k-1 shared bits are all 1 and the private parity is 1, giving influence 2^{-(k-1)}/2=delta. Thus J=r delta, I_Z(F)=k delta, I(F)=(r+k)delta. Each module also has shared influence k delta. Its unfurled repeated loss is k(r-1)delta.

For coordinate i let c_i be the number of module characters containing i. Character multiplication complements its module influence when present. Therefore

L(C)=sum_i [(r-1)delta+(c_i-(c_i mod 2))(1-2delta)].

Since delta<=1/2 and c_i-(c_i mod2)>=0, the exact minimum over ALL module shared-character choices is k(r-1)delta. Equality is attained with no characters. This is an actual global-character minimization; context-dependent nonlinear gauges are not included and need not preserve H.

Taking r=k^2 gives min L/I(F)=k-1, min L/J=k-1/k, and I(F)=(k^2+k)2^-k tending to zero. Hence no absolute constant pays this repeated loss with either private or whole influence. The small exact witness k=2,r=3 already has min L=1>J=3/4.

The algebraic normal form of the sign exponent is sum_j (product_i Z_i)y_j. Each monomial contains every shared variable and one private variable, so the variable-interaction hypergraph is connected. A sign tensor factorization over a proper partition would express this unique ANF as a sum of polynomials on separate variable blocks, which these crossing monomials prohibit. Adding any global character changes only linear monomials. Thus spectator removal or exact physical tensor factorization does not remove the obstruction.

### What repeated one-coordinate payment actually proves

For general modules sharing k bits, fix coordinate i and context w=Z without i. Fold each conditional one-bit module edge separately. The reviewed theorem gives ell_i(w)=sum_j delta_(j,i)(w)-delta_*,i(w)<=sum_j J_(j,i)(w)-delta_*,i(w). Averaging contexts gives E sum_j J_(j,i)=J for EACH i. Consequently sum_i E ell_i<=kJ-sum_i E delta_*,i. Our family attains equality: only the all-one context contributes, where every delta_j=J_j=1/2 and delta_*=1/2. The width factor k is unavoidable for this diagnostic. Conditional folding need not integrate to one entropy-preserving global character; it is not a general closure theorem. In our family no negative correlations occur, so the diagnostic agrees with the actual minimum character loss.

### Full normalized joint reference and exact spectral refund

Let chi_Y=(-1)^(xor_j y_j). Then F=1-A+A chi_Y. The coefficient at the empty set is 1-delta. Every other set in the family {shared subset} times {empty private subset, all private bits} has coefficient magnitude delta. All other coefficients vanish. There are 2^{k+1}-1 such nonempty sets. Let epsilon=2delta-delta^2; their total mass is epsilon.

An explicit parameter-fixed complete reference assigns Q(empty)=(1-delta)^2 and Q(S)=delta^2 to each of those nonempty sets, zero elsewhere. It is normalized since (1-delta)^2+(2^{k+1}-1)delta^2=1. A complete decoder distinguishes the empty set and enumerates the shared subset and private-parity flag. This Q is given by the family formula, not a generic oracle for an unknown spectrum. Its full CE equals

H_k=-(1-delta)^2 log2((1-delta)^2)+2k epsilon.

Using -(1-epsilon)ln(1-epsilon)<=epsilon and ln2>1/2,

H_k<=(2k+1/ln2)epsilon<=(4k+2/ln2)delta<4(k+1)delta<=4I(F).

Each module has the same entropy H_k, because replacing its private bit by the parity of all private bits embeds the same Fourier probability law. Hence shared multiplication refunds exactly (r-1)H_k. A bound on influence loss alone cannot discard this spectral refund. These are FEI controls and payment diagnostics, not new universal entropy closure.

### Exact shared-block-first tree has no K4 bad-node bill

Query shared bits in any order; a zero answer makes the remaining function constant +1, and the all-one terminal is the private parity character. Every terminal has entropy zero and a complete point-mass reference of cost zero; its remaining private influence is retained, not charged again.

At an all-one prefix with ell shared bits remaining, let d=2^-ell. The exact erasure B for the next shared query is

B=q_0 h2(d^2/q_0)+2d-2d^2,  q_0=(1-d)^2+d^2.

This follows by pairing the two selector Fourier sectors: the empty erased row has weights (1-d)^2,d^2, and the other 2^ell-1 erased rows each have equal weights d^2,d^2. The entropy inequality q h2(a/q)<=a log2(q/a)+a/ln2 and q_0<=1 give

B<=2d+d^2(2ell-2+1/ln2)<3d,

since 2ell d<=1 and 1/ln2<2. Thus every visited shared query is K4 good and the localized bad-node bill is exactly zero. At every depth the visit-weight times conditional influence equals delta, so consumed shared influence totals k delta. The reviewed JS bound M<=2d/ln2 then also bounds the full entropy telescope by (3+2/ln2)k delta with zero terminal CE. The sharper explicit joint reference above supplies CE<=4I directly. This family exposes a failure of repeated module ownership, while supporting a successful adaptive shared-block policy.

### Honest remaining gap

The first unsupported universal line is existence, for arbitrary unequal or overlapping shared phase masks and supplied terminal references, of a dimension-free joint refund/owned-reserve inequality that pays the signed collision excess without spending each J_j once per shared coordinate. Neither the formula family nor the conditional width-k diagnostic proves that line. A018's averaged paired-row inequality is not used, proved or refuted here. The adaptive C019 localized stopping-tree theorem remains open.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

The module core is g=1 for x=0 and g=h for x=1, edited on E={x=1} according to the sign mask. Write a=I(h), J=I_private(F), d=I_shared(F), T=sum over the prescribed z-then-w prefix of reach weight times 2I(D). The test is whether positive internal-pivotal correction W forces a positive localized K4 bill or makes the raw rate4 sufficient ledger necessary. Both diagnostic implications are false. We do not assert a universal payment for arbitrary nonbalanced terminal cores.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### Independent reconstruction of A019's account

For arbitrary core g and edit E set b=g(-1)^1_E and D=g1_E. A directed private edge contributes (u(x)-u(x^i))^2/(4*2^m) to real Dirichlet energy. On an internal pivotal edge, J and kappa receive the same amount, leaving its original pivotal contribution in 2kappa-J. On an external pivotal edge, kappa is zero and J receives its original amount. On a crossing edge, the two children have complementary pivotal status, so J contributes 1/(2*2^m) and kappa contributes 1/(4*2^m). Crossing terms cancel. Hence exactly 2kappa-J=P_in-P_out. This establishes the iff half-private condition, and independent edit sets have P_in=0. A point edit has kappa=m delta/2. If r original pivotal directions meet that point, I(g)>=2r delta and J=I(g)+delta(m-2r)>=m delta.

On each shared edge, independent private blocks give C_j=sum_U |U| ahat_j(U)bhat_j(U) and exact product derivative energy sum_j[J_j-Q_-j C_j]/2. Substituting 2kappa_j=J_j-C_j yields t_j=J_j/2+Q_-j(P_in-P_out)_j/2. An unchanged edit means effective E is empty and its correction is -J_j, not zero. Density<=1/2 makes every Q_-j nonnegative. Under P_in<=P_out, averaging two directions gives u_j<=J_j with one module account. Parseval gives T_private=2sum u_j=2J+W and T=T_private+2E_zw. Since 2E_zw<=d, T<=2J+d. Filtering nonnegative charges only decreases T.

For supplied normalized four-leaf references Q_b let Qbar=average Q_b, R=average actual terminal spectral laws, and use the actual parent shared conditional law times Qbar as the root reference. Normalization and log-sum give root KL<=average terminal KL; therefore CE_root<=cbar+Delta_block H. The accepted B04-NODE-BOUNDARY@1 telescopes to Delta_block H<=T+(3/ln2)d. All terminal costs remain. The claimed conditional transfer max(C+2,1+3/ln2) follows from cbar<=CJ. The source's two-bit witness g=[1,1,-1,1], E={0,2} gives J=1, kappa=3/4, P_in=1/2,P_out=0. Thus all parts of A019's theorem reconstruct without repair, with its accepted node-boundary dependency retained for CE only. Independent finite edge-partition checks cover every core/edit pair on one and two bits (272 pairs). No universal reference or recursive slack supply is inferred.

### Exact spectral split of the balanced private gate

Use sign character chi_x=(-1)^x. The identity is F=(1+chi_x)/2+(1-chi_x)h s/2. Balance gives hhat(empty)=0, so these two parts have disjoint physical Fourier labels. The first part has two coefficients 1/2 at empty and {x}. Every nonempty private y-set Y has coefficients plus/minus hhat(Y) shat(L)/2 with x absent/present. Consequently the full squared law has two constant-sector atoms of mass1/4 and two copies of P_h(Y)P_s(L)/4. It is normalized exactly.

The complete entropy and physical influences are

H(F)=2+[H(h)+H(s)]/2,
J=(a+1)/2, d=I(s)/2, I(F)=(a+1+I(s))/2.

Each actual terminal has H=2+H(h)/2; its sign s only shifts the branch signs and does not change its probability law. Thus the full insertion is H(s)/2. Given supplied complete references Q_h,Q_s, a normalized full reference puts mass1/4 on each constant atom and adds Q_h(Y)Q_s(L)/4 on both x copies, combining coincident atoms when Y is empty. Its complete CE is <=2+(c_h+c_s)/2. In particular using the explicitly known true laws in this specified family gives equality with H(F). With c_h<=C_h a and c_s<=C_s I(s), the transfer rate is max(4,C_h,C_s). This is a transparent calibration using supplied terminals, not a novel universal reference construction.

### Exact erasure and the positive correction

For either shared coordinate i, conditional erasure is exactly B_i(F)=B_i(s)/2, since the constant sector contains no shared frequency and each h-row merely repeats the mask law. Any two-bit sign mask is either an affine character (eight cases, B_i=0) or a one-point defect/complement (eight cases, four equal squared coefficients, B_i=1 and Inf_i=1/2). Thus B_i(s)<=2Inf_i(s) exactly. After the first shared query its remaining one-bit mask is a constant or character; every second shared query has B=0. Therefore this prescribed shared-first prefix has zero exact K2, hence K4, bad-node bill for EVERY balanced h and two-bit mask, regardless of positive W. This proof does not estimate B by h2 of disagreement.

On a changed shared edge the derivative is plus/minus 1_{x=1}h, whose private energy is kappa=a/2+1/4. The two child private energies give J=(a+1)/2. Internal pivotal contribution is a/2 and external pivotal contribution zero, so 2kappa-J=a/2>0. An unchanged edge has kappa=0. Direct spectral expansion gives

T_private=(a+1/2)I(s), E_zw=Phat_s({z,w})/2,
T=(a+1/2)I(s)+Phat_s({z,w}),
W=(a+1/2)I(s)-(a+1).

Here Phat_s denotes squared Fourier probability, not amplitude. For s=chi_z chi_w, I(s)=2 and Phat_s({z,w})=1. Thus W=a, T=2a+2=4J, d=1, B=0 at all shared queries, and the actual insertion H(s)/2 is zero. Taking h to be parity on m private bits gives a=m, H(h)=0, terminal cbar=2 and a fully explicit four-atom root reference of CE=2. Its raw unfiltered rate4 ledger has exact deficit

[cbar+T+(3/ln2)d]-4I(F)=3/ln2-2>0.

So that sufficient inequality fails for every m despite complete CE=2<=4I. This refutes using the raw signed correction or the unfiltered sufficient test as a necessary payment obligation. It does not refute A019's conditional sufficient theorem, whose assumptions on half-private editing fail in this example. The correct K4 localized tree has zero bill and retains the actual terminal reference.

### All-width connected zero-erasure control

Replace s by parity of k>=1 shared bits and h by parity of m>=1 private bits. The ANF exponent is x times the sum of every private/shared bit. Its interaction graph is a star through x, hence connected and admits no proper disjoint physical sign tensor factorization. The squared spectrum consists of four atoms of mass1/4: empty, x, all m+k parity bits, and that set plus x. Therefore H=CE=2, every shared and parity-private coordinate has influence1/2, and the gate has influence1/2. I=(m+k+1)/2. Every shared query is erasure-free; all reached descendants have the same property until the terminal gate/parity function. The gate itself has B=1 and Inf_x=1/2, so a complete physical tree may also be chosen K2 good.

The raw derivative prefix querying all k shared coordinates charges the private degree and remaining shared coordinates on the parity sector. Summing exactly gives

T_raw=k m+k^2/2,
T_raw/I=(2km+k^2)/(m+k+1).

For m=k^2 this ratio grows as 2k, while the exact K4 localized bill stays zero and full CE stays2. This is a scoped calibration for keeping the exact bad-node filter; fixed-order second-moment growth was already known and is not advertised as a new general no-go. It is distinct from stage3's repeated-owner example: here the internal-pivotal correction explicitly violates half-private and supports the newly consumed A019 diagnostic.

### Nonbalanced inherited cores: parity mask still pays

For ANY Boolean h, let beta=E h and let s be parity of k shared bits. The shared-empty constant sector and the shared-full h sector remain disjoint even when h has an empty coefficient. Thus H(F)=2+H(h)/2, I(F)=(I(h)+k+1)/2, and a supplied complete h reference of cost c_h gives a normalized complete root reference of CE=2+c_h/2: put1/4 at empty and x, and Q_h(Y)/4 at each of the two shared-full x copies. Every shared query except the last has B=0, because empty and full shared supports differ on at least two unqueried coordinates. At the last shared query only the empty h row interferes. Its exact erasure is

B_last=(1+beta^2)/2*h2(beta^2/(1+beta^2))<=1,

whereas conditional influence is1/2. Hence the entire shared-first prefix is K2 good for every arbitrary, possibly biased private core, and both terminal gate/core costs remain supplied. If c_h<=C I(h), this particular reference is paid at max(C,4)I(F). This is a scoped parity control, not a supply of c_h for arbitrary cores. The arbitrary-core raw prefix still has T=k I(h)+k^2/2. Balance loss alone therefore does not produce a debt for a character shared mask.

### Honest first unsupported line

Balance is doing essential work in the arbitrary nonlinear mask identity: it separates the constant-sector private rows from the inherited h rows. When BOTH hhat(empty) and shat(empty) are nonzero, those rows interfere, so neither B_i(F)=B_i(s)/2 nor the exact constant entropy split may be asserted. The first unsupported next line is a paid bound on that coincident empty-row interference for a nonlinear shared mask against one supplied complete terminal reference and one unused private/core reserve after a data restriction destroys balance. The parity case just proved is an exception and must remain a mandatory control. Positive W by itself cannot identify the debt, and a raw derivative bound misses exact zero erasure. Arbitrary unequal multi-module masks and adaptive nonbalanced child-core payment remain open. A018's false averaged shortcut is not revived.

# A020 stage 5: a true-positive row defeats the balanced-child reference repair

Full FEI remains OPEN. Nearly identical biased children defeat the extension of A019's balanced-child zero-row repair: an essential query of disagreement 2^-k has net reference surcharge bounded below by1/32 bit. ## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

All bits are independent uniform 0/1 bits, chi_b=(-1)^b, outputs Boolean signs, entropy/CE/KL in bits. For every k>=3 let v be k private bits, A=1_{v=0}, delta=2^-k, and define an essential private query q and another private bit u by

h(q,u,v)=chi_u(1-A)+chi_q A.

Its children are h_0=chi_u+(1-chi_u)A and h_1=chi_u-(1+chi_u)A. Let s on two fresh shared bits be a sign point defect with mean1/2, so all four squared Fourier coefficients are1/4. Form F=1 on private gate x0 and h*s on x1. All child/core/activation references below are specified and normalized before spectral sampling.

Supply Q_b=(P_b+delta_empty)/2 to child h_b. Incoming Q_h is the actual queried-frequency conditional lift of average(Q_0,Q_1), with queried frequency absent on ZERO true unqueried rows. Use the SAME Q_s=P_s at all nodes. Activation uses the joint one-physical-label reference of A018: data prior (Q_h tensor Q_s+delta_empty)/2, lifted by actual gate-frequency conditionals. Its posterior-label KL refund is D. Define D_core=avg child core KL-parent core KL and Lambda=avg D_child-D_parent-D_core/2. The false diagnostic is Lambda<=K Pr(h_0!=h_1) for an absolute K under this support-aware rule without the balanced-child hypothesis. The failure also holds when pricing by actual F-query influence, which is half that disagreement.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Independent reconstruction of the source reference clauses

For a sign G with mean mu, activation F=1 at x0 and G at x1 has coefficients(1+mu)/2,(1-mu)/2 on the empty data row and two Ghat(T)/2 coefficients on every nonempty row. Thus H(F)=H(G)/2+Psi(mu), where

Psi(mu)=eta((1+mu)^2/4)+eta((1-mu)^2/4)+1-mu^2-eta(mu^2)/2,
eta(t)=-t log2 t.

The erased data law is R=(P_G+delta_empty)/2. With the same physical-label prior r_Q=(Q_G+delta_empty)/2 and true parent conditional gate law, parent KL is KL(R||r_Q). The tagged branch KL is KL(P_G||Q_G)/2. Chain rule gives their difference D>=0, concentrated in the empty data row:

D=(1+mu^2)/2 KL_Ber(mu^2/(1+mu^2) || t/(1+t)), t=Q_G(empty).

Therefore CE_F=c_G/2+Psi(mu)-D. With independent h and s, c_G=c_h+c_s and mu=beta sigma. Expanding the binary expression gives, in natural units,

D_nat=[mu^2 ln(mu^2/t)+(1+mu^2)ln((1+t)/(1+mu^2))]/2,

using the continuous zero conventions. This identity is reconstructed from the tagged law rather than imported from an author implementation.

For a core query with consistent incoming conditional-mixture Q_h, log-sum gives D_core=avg child KL-parent KL>=0 and c_h=avg c_b+Delta H_core-D_core. Substitution yields exactly

CE_parent-avg CE_child=Delta H_core/2+curvature+Lambda,
Lambda=avg D_child-D_parent-D_core/2.

If both children are balanced, the parent empty UNQUERIED row has zero true mass. Assigning queried frequency absent there gives Q_h(empty)=avg Q_b(empty). All activation refunds become (1/2)log2(1+t_s Q_h(empty)), t_s=Q_s(empty). Concavity of this log gives avg D_child<=D_parent, hence Lambda<=0. If instead splitting the zero row equally, the source family h0=chi_u,h1=chi_u times a private point phase, Q_b=(P_b+delta_empty)/2, gives Lambda=(1/2)log2(18/17), D_core=0. Our independent helper reproduces both conventions on k3..8 actual Boolean fixtures. Review verdict: PARTIAL HOLDS for the exact reference/insertion clauses, all-dimensional balanced-child repair and specified equal-split no-go. This review excludes the sharp curvature optimum and the source's complete sparse-family rate4 eligibility proof; no substantive repair is supplied and the original claim statement/hash are preserved.

### 2. Nearly identical children with opposite tiny means

On A=0 both children equal chi_u. On A=1 child0 is constant+1 and child1 constant-1. Thus their physical disagreement is exactly delta, and E h_0=delta, E h_1=-delta, E h=0. They are nonconstant and biased for every k>=3. They differ on the rare entire u-fiber; closeness does not make the interfering Fourier row vanish.

The Fourier coefficients of A on v are all delta. Child0 has coefficient delta on every set with u absent, coefficient-delta on every nonempty v set with u present, and main coefficient1-delta at{u}. Child1 reverses exactly the u-absent coefficients. Hence P_0=P_1=P, with P(empty)=delta^2. Each has one squared mass(1-delta)^2 and2^{k+1}-1 squared masses delta^2. The parent replaces each u-absent child frequency by that same set with q PRESENT; the u-present frequencies have q absent. This is a relabeling of the NONZERO law, so H(h)=H(h_b)=H_k, with additional zero query rows explicitly retained in reference normalization.

Every unqueried row has only one true q sector. Therefore its erased law is exactly P and the parent reference assigns the same Q=(P+delta_empty)/2 to that sector. Child and parent core KL are identically

K_core=1+delta^2 log2(delta^2/(1+delta^2))<=1.

Thus D_core=0, Delta H_core=0. In particular the empty UNQUERIED row has positive true massdelta^2, all at q present. Its reference mass(1+delta^2)/2 is FORCED to q present. Parent full-core empty-set reference mass is zero, while each child's empty-set reference is(1+delta^2)/2. No convention on zero true rows can alter this positive row while retaining the stipulated actual conditional lift.

### 3. A fixed positive net refund loss

The parent mean is zero and Q_h(empty)=0, hence D_parent=0. At either child mu^2=delta^2/4=:a and t=Q_b(empty)Q_s(empty)=(1+delta^2)/8. Both child refunds are equal, so Lambda=D_child and

Lambda_nat=[a ln(a/t)+(1+a)ln((1+t)/(1+a))]/2.

As k tends to infinity this converges to(1/2)ln(9/8), so Lambda tends to(1/2)log2(9/8)>0. A uniform explicit sign suffices. For k>=3, a<=1/256, t>=1/8 and t<=1. The first term is at least a ln a. On that interval -a ln a<=ln256/256<1/32. Further (1+a)ln(1+a)<=2a<=1/128, while (1+a)ln(1+t)>=ln(9/8)>=1/9. Therefore

Lambda_nat>(1/2)(1/9-5/128)>1/32.

Since ln2<1, Lambda_bits>1/32 as well. The upper bound Lambda<=K_core/2<=1/2 follows from the tagged KL identity, because Q_s is exact. Consequently Lambda/d>2^k/32 diverges. The actual F-query influence is d/2, so the failure is stronger in those units. This refutes the proposed balanced-child-free query-price extension, not the stated balanced-child theorem.

### 4. The actual entropy query is K1 good

Actual entropy insertion is only curvature=Psi(0)-Psi(delta/2), because Delta H_core=0. A parent F row has mixed q sectors only on the empty unqueried core/shared row. The two gate-frequency copies each have weights1/4 anddelta^2/16=a/4. Therefore exact query erasure is

B_q(F)=(1+a)/2 h2(a/(1+a)).

Using minority entropy bound and ln2>1/2,

B_q<=a[ (1/2)log2((1+a)/a)+1/(2ln2)]
<a(k+5/2)=delta^2(k+5/2)/4.

At k3, delta(k+5/2)=11/16<2, and this decreases with k. Hence B_q<delta/2=Inf_q(F): the query is K1 good, and its K4 localized derivative bill is zero. Positive Lambda is a reference-transport debt, not an unpaid actual entropy or raw derivative necessity.

For completeness, the prescribed two-shared-bit mask prefix is also K4 good for ANY h: a nonzero shared mask influence is at least1/2, so its influence in F is at least1/4 and B_i<=1<=4Inf_i; on each remaining one-bit child it is0 or1/2. Zero influence implies absent selector coefficients. This elementary finite-width guard is not a new high-influence closure result.

### 5. All references are eligible; one owned terminal bank pays once

Direct physical edges give I(h_b)=1+(k-1)delta and I(h)=1+k delta. For child0, flipping its exceptional point removes one original u-pivotal edge and adds one in each of k private-v directions, yielding the stated correction; the parent uses q on A and u elsewhere and has each v-direction influence delta.

The common spectral entropy is

H_k=-(1-delta)^2 log2((1-delta)^2)+(2^{k+1}-1)delta^2(2k).

Writing epsilon=2delta-delta^2, the inequality -(1-epsilon)ln(1-epsilon)<=epsilon and ln2>1/2 give H_k<4(k+1)delta. Thus c_b=H_k+K_core<=H_k+1, so the child's certified rate4 slack satisfies

S_b=4I(h_b)-c_b>3-8delta>=2.

Parent slack is similarly>3-4delta>=5/2. These are complete references with substantial unused budgets, not unsupported ghost distributions. Full activation references are also rate4: Psi is maximized at0 with value2 (differentiate the displayed expression or use concavity), so CE_F<=c_h/2+3<7/2+2(k+1)delta. Parent I(F)=(3+kdelta)/2, giving margin at least5/2-2delta. Child I(F_b)=[3+(k-1)delta minus/plus delta/2]/2, giving margin at least5/2-5delta>0. All three actual full-reference costs are retained in the exact insertion identity, not replaced by root-only entropy.

To name the one-use payment, each child's unused PRIVATE core half-budget is S_b/2>=1 after paying c_b/2. Mask code costs c_s/2=1 from its rate4 mask half-budget2, leaving1. The gate profile costs at most2 from gate budget2 minus/plus delta; the potential shortfall at the positive-mean child is at mostdelta and can use the mask's remaining1, leaving the private half-budget untouched. The actual insertion has no core-entropy debit and is Lambda+curvature. A019's scalar inequality (or the elementary bound below) gives curvature<delta. Together with Lambda<=1/2 and delta<=1/8, the debit is<5/8, paid once from the averaged untouched private half-reserve>=1, leaving>3/8. No positive core KL refund is claimed, no new bank is created after this query, and no future recursion is authorized by this one-step payment.

For a self-contained coarse curvature bound in this family, the actual private query entropy insertion is B_q+JS_q. The previously independently accepted JS<=2Inf_q/ln2 yields a linear bound with a finite constant, but the sharper curvature<delta can also be read directly from the elementary concavity chord argument for phi(b)=Psi(b/2): -phi''(b)=(1/4)log2((4-b^2)/b^2) is even decreasing in|b|, so its symmetric Jensen gap at child means+/-delta is bounded by delta[phi(0)-phi(1)]=delta[(9/8)log2(3)-1]<delta. Only this elementary bound is needed for the named bank; the source sharp optimum is not independently accepted as part of the review.

### 6. What fails and what remains unsupported

Unlike A019's equal-split failure, the row here is true-positive and uniquely forces query-present reference mass. The balanced-child absent-frequency repair is not robust under arbitrarily small nonzero opposite means. Discarding Lambda, pricing it solely by removed query influence, or treating the zero-row convention as a repair on positive true rows is false. All existing proved claims remain valid at their stated scopes.

The first unsupported universal line is an inherited reference policy or conserved certified child bank that pays successive positive Lambda terms on multiple biased-child exposures without renewing the same half-core slack. This family has enough unused slack for one paid insertion; it does not imply such slack always exists or telescopes through arbitrary later restrictions. NEXT tests a second essential query of a v coordinate while both core branches remain nonconstant, carrying this EXACT incoming Q_h and its booked debit. A new conditional-mixture reset or fresh exact-spectrum reference would change the question and is not permitted.
