## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The full proof is in BIAS_TRANSPORT.tex; BOUNDARY_CREDITS.md gives analytic endpoint expansions and their uniformity limits. Let Eplus denote the corrected entropy, D=I-Var, e(u)=u log2(1/u), m_i=E f_i, v_i=1-m_i^2, and mu the actual product law of child outputs. The composing definition is

    Eplus_mu(g)=sum_{S nonempty} P_mu(S) log2(product_{i in S} v_i/P_mu(S)).

There is no output-variance numerator. For physical uniform f, H(f)=Eplus(f)+e((E f)^2). Conditional nonempty entropy differs by the exact v log2(1/v) normalization term.

Set a_i=E_{mu_-i}(D_i g)^2, a bounded pivotal probability. For v_i>0 it equals Inf_i^mu(g)/v_i; at v_i=0 use contraction, and the polynomial a_i only as an extension for zero-weight inner contributions. The known unique-disjoint-union Fourier identity gives

    Eplus(F)=Eplus_mu(g)+sum_i a_i Eplus(f_i),
    I(F)=sum_i a_i I(f_i),
    D(F)=D_mu(g)+sum_i a_i D(f_i).                         (1)

After querying x inside j let delta Z=Z-(Z+ + Z-)/2, beta=(m_j+ - m_j-)/2, d=Inf_x(f_j), t=a_j, b=E D_j g, M=E F. The remaining physical inputs stay uniform. Only the output law changes. For i!=j, a_i is affine in m_j, hence its child average equals its original value; t is independent of m_j. Consequently

    delta Eplus(F)=delta Eplus_mu(g)+t delta Eplus(f_j),
    delta I(F)=t d,
    delta D_mu(g)=beta^2(t-b^2),
    delta D(F)=t d-b^2 beta^2.                            (2)

The variance calculation uses delta Var(f_j)=beta^2 and delta Var(F)=b^2 beta^2. Define

    T=delta Eplus_mu(g)+delta e(M^2)-t delta e(m_j^2).

Then the exact full-FEI surplus transfer is

    delta H(F)=t delta H(f_j)+T,
    delta(C I-H)(F)=t delta(C I-H)(f_j)-T.                (3)

The C(D)-Eplus slack has its own cocycle; the empty-atom reserve C Var-e(mean^2) converts it to full-FEI slack. C beta^2(t-b^2) is not free additional influence: it cancels in that conversion.

For the directly physical representation F=A(z)+B(z)f_j(u), Booleanity gives AB=0 and A^2+B^2=1 pointwise, with t=E B^2. Define L(a,c)=e(a^2)+e(c^2)-e(a^2+c^2). The exact erasure transfer is

    B_x(F)=t B_x(f_j)+R,
    R=sum_Z L(Ahat_Z+m_j Bhat_Z,beta Bhat_Z)-t L(m_j,beta). (4)

Each nonempty residual inner set contributes by homogeneity exactly t times its inner erasure cost. Only the inner empty residual set changes: its amplitude becomes Ahat+m_j Bhat. Removing its old contribution proves the negative subtraction in (4). This fills A057's stated descendant gap for arbitrary means and deterministic endpoints. With the actual physical JS terms,

    T=R+JS_x(F)-t JS_x(f_j).                              (5)

For t>0, beta!=0, scalar binary entropy and log-sum also give

    R <= t beta^2 log2([1-t+(m_j^2+beta^2)t]/[t beta^2])
         +t beta^2 log2(e)-t L(m_j,beta).                 (6)

Here sum_Z(Ahat+m_j Bhat)^2=1-t+m_j^2 t. The displayed bound leaves outer rarity t visible; it does not assert R<=K t d.

Retain the existing mismatch rebate using actual inner children: q_U=(fhat_j+(U)^2+fhat_j-(U)^2)/2, r_U=(|fhat_j+(U)|-|fhat_j-(U)|)^2/4, eta=sum r_U, nu=r/eta. For eta>0,

    delta H(F) <= t eta[log2(1/eta)-KL_2(nu||q)+3/ln2]+T. (7)

At eta=0 the inner decrement vanishes exactly; T need not vanish. Dropping KL or replacing the actual composed children by inner children would be invalid.

At beta=0, all output laws are unchanged and T=R=0: the entire decrement transports by t. For a signed dictator outer gate T=0 at every endpoint. Contract deterministic outputs before forming a normalized basis or dividing by a deficit. Corrected entropy cancels the outer v_j log(1/v_j) term exactly. For a fixed finite gate and fixed other biases, its corrected endpoint drift is O(v_j), with possible second-order v_j^2 log(1/v_j) terms from newly zero normalized coefficients; this is not an arity-uniform remainder.

The log(1/mean) requested by the lead appears in the scalar subtraction. At fixed nonzero m and beta->0, if Z(m)=sum_{Ahat+m Bhat=0} Bhat^2 then R=-Z(m) beta^2 log(1/beta^2)+O(beta^2); its leading rare-beta logarithm is nonpositive. At m=0, R=kappa beta^2 log(1/beta^2)+O(beta^2), kappa=sum_{Ahat!=0} Bhat^2<=t. In the iterated regime beta=o(|m|), m->0, the beta^2 coefficient has kappa log(1/m^2)+O(1). The exact subtraction resolves this nonuniform crossover. This logarithmic component is bounded by [2/(e ln2)]t d, since |beta|<=d and u log(1/u)<=1/e. It is A057's affordable bank, not the whole erasure payment or a claim that all nodes are good. Bad-node status requires the full comparison B_x(F)>K t d.

A near-dictator calibration demonstrates the missing credit: f_j=x(1-2 1_{z=0}) with r control bits and a two-input AND outer with another fair literal. At query x, eta_inner=0 and delta H(f_j)=0; t=1/2 and delta I(F)=1/2, but delta H(F)=T tends to 2 bits (R tends to 1 and JS_F to 1). Explicit point-defect spectral masses give the vanishing entropy remainder O(r 2^-r). This is a constant-price calibration, not an unbounded bad-node family.

**First unsupported universal line:** a stopping-tree bound on the entire signed outer residual sum plus the weighted inner KL-log charges and terminal entropy by an absolute multiple of root physical influence. Neither (6), small inner mismatch, nor bounded a_i establishes it. Outer-rarity logs cannot be charged repeatedly against ancestral influence. No universal theorem or FEI counterfamily is obtained.

# A025: strict threshold 2 fails localized odd-face payment

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI remains OPEN. The B05 lead's v1 instruction replaces the already retired local variance-refund helper by

Q_bad,K(T) := sum_{v: B_{i_v}(f_v)>K d_v} pi_v I(g_v h_v)/2 <= Kprime I(f),

where g_v,h_v are the actual fair Boolean children, d_v=Inf_{i_v}(f_v), B_i=H(S_i|S_-i), and pi_v is the physical probability of reaching v. All entropies are in bits. This stage proves failure for K=2 (and K<2), even for every complete adaptive tree of its explicit family. It does not refute the existence of a sufficiently large K,Kprime. We use {0,1} coordinates as a relabeling of the uniform sign cube. The selected family has output rarity 2^-n and dimension n.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json was read in full, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2. Read campaign README/status, registry/obstruction and packet/next-action navigation, the plan's B05/A025 contracts, the supplemental campaign, claims, counterexamples, seed lemmas and mapping. B05's original 20261011T193057Z report sections 2-7 and B19's 20261011T195038Z audit were read to retire their accepted local no-go; it is not reproved or used as a premise. The B09 earlier report was read as background, not accepted as a dependency. Read ERASURE_REDUCTION.md sections 1-5 and BLOCK_RESTRICTION_IDENTITY sections 1-3 for notation and scope. The threshold 38pp paper introduction, composition and rare-activation theorem locators, its 29pp earlier version, and the percolation 30pp/31pp introductions and theorem locators were consulted; no result from either paper is needed here. The older PDF text exports contain legacy non-UTF8 bytes; replacement decoding was used after an initial decoding failure.

No source-complete B05 lead REPORT.md existed under its run tree when checked. Therefore the face recurrence below is reconstructed, rather than relying on the lead's in-progress claim. Exact local SHA256 receipts and read scope are in out/SOURCES_READ.json. No web source or priority claim is needed for this self-contained derivation.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Let f_n(x)=1-2*1_{x=0}. This is a genuine Boolean sign, equal to -1 at one vertex only. Put q=2^-n. Its Fourier coefficients are a_empty=1-2q and a_S=-2q for every nonempty S. Each coordinate has influence d=2q, so I(f_n)=2nq.

At any split of a live m-variable point-defect node, one child is constant +1 and one is f_{m-1}, up to translation. Hence their product is f_{m-1}, and

c_m := I(g h)/2 = (m-1)2^(1-m).

The spectra show that each nonempty residual Fourier pair has equal masses 4q_m^2, where q_m=2^-m. There are 2^(m-1)-1 such pairs. Only the empty residual pair is exceptional, with masses A=(1-2q_m)^2 and D=4q_m^2. Consequently

B_i(f_m)=4q_m-8q_m^2+E(A,D),
E(A,D)=A log2(1+D/A)+D log2(1+A/D).

For m>=3, A/D=(2^(m-1)-1)^2>=9. Thus

E(A,D)>D log2(A/D)>=4q_m^2 log2(9)>8q_m^2,

so B_i(f_m)>4q_m=2d for every coordinate. At m=2 the spectrum is uniform on four sets and B_i=1=2d exactly; at m=1 the function is a character and B_i=0. Therefore the strict K=2 bad nodes are exactly the live m>=3 nodes.

Any adaptive complete physical tree has exactly one live path; every restriction along it remains a translated point defect. The probability of reaching its m-variable live node is 2^-(n-m), irrespective of coordinate order. All other reached nodes are constant and have zero charge. Thus, for every such tree and every n>=3,

Q_bad,2(T)=sum_{m=3}^n 2^-(n-m)c_m
          =2^(1-n)[n(n-1)/2-1],
Q_bad,2(T)/I(f_n)=(n-1)/2-1/n -> infinity.

For any fixed Kprime>=0, every integer n>=ceil(2Kprime+3) yields a strict violation. If K<2, the m=2 node is also bad, and Q_bad,K/I=(n-1)/2. This is a counterfamily to a threshold choice and its claimed linear global charge, not merely a finite failed coefficient.

To check the face normalization independently, define Q(f)=2^(1-n) times the number of 2-faces with corner product -1. Faces not involving the split coordinate partition into the two child cubes and contribute their average Q. A face involving that coordinate is odd exactly when its residual edge has different values of gh. The number of such edges is 2^(n-2) I(gh), so their normalized contribution is I(gh)/2. This proves Q(f)=I(gh)/2+(Q(g)+Q(h))/2. For f_n, precisely the binomial(n,2) faces containing its exceptional vertex are odd. The total Q/I=(n-1)/2 agrees with the localized formula after subtracting the single m=2 payment 1/n.

The threshold boundary is essential. Since E(A,D)<=D log2(e(A+D)/D) and A+D<=1 for m>=2,

B_i(f_m)/d <= 2+2^-m(4m-8+2log2(e)) < 2+4m2^-m.

Here the entropy estimate follows from -(1-t)ln(1-t)<=t with t=D/(A+D). For fixed K>2, let M_K be the largest integer m>=2 satisfying 4m2^-m>K-2, or take M_K=1 if that set is empty. It is finite. Only nodes with m<=M_K can be bad. Thus every complete tree of this family satisfies

Q_bad,K(T)/I(f_n) <= M_K(M_K-1)/(2n) -> 0.

This family supplies no large-K obstruction. It also satisfies H(f_n)/I(f_n)<4 for n>=2: its tail entropy is (2^n-1)4q^2(2n-2), while the empty atom entropy is <=(4q-4q^2)log2(e). After division by 2nq the sum is <4-4/n+2log2(e)/n<4. Its Shannon ratio tends to 4, consistent with the campaign's point-defect controls.

No line in the stated threshold-2 theorem remains unsupported. The first unpaid line for the intended universal route is: there exist sufficiently large fixed K,Kprime such that the localized payment holds on arbitrary Boolean functions for an appropriately quantified physical tree, with terminal entropy retained. This stage neither proves nor disproves that line. A stopping tree may terminate before all coordinates are read; the theorem does not claim that every stopping strategy fails.

## executed validation with domain, arithmetic, commands and outputs

code/exact_tree.py exhausts all Boolean functions in n=0..4 modulo output complement. It computes integer Walsh transforms and exact d, verifies Parseval and the influence identity, classifies B_i>Kd by exact integer-power comparison, and uses dynamic programming over every coordinate choice and both actual children to maximize the complete-tree localized cost. Coordinates are not discarded by a heuristic. Output complement preserves spectra and gh, so that quotient is exact.

For integer Walsh coefficients a_S and N=2^n, set A=a_T^2 and D=a_{T+i}^2. Multiplying (A+D)^(A+D)/(A^A D^D) over pairs and comparing with 2^(K sum D) exactly certifies the strict logarithmic sign. Zero entries contribute a factor one. The physical cost at a node is the directed edge-count of gh divided by N. In the DP, its integer numerator adds to both child numerators because their fair 1/2 weights cancel the halved child cube size. Therefore the DP returns exact rational cost/influence ratios.

Frozen spec: out/FROZEN_SPEC.json. Its original written count 32898 was an arithmetic typo; the immutable correction out/SPEC_CORRECTION.json records 1+2+8+128+32768=32907, with no changed domain or dimension escalation. Actual census: 32907 cases, 197286 exact assertions, 3.8322 seconds. Maximum ratios by dimension are:

| n | K=2 | K=3 | K=4 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| 0,1,2 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| 3 | 2/3 | 0 | 0 |
| 4 | 5/4 | 3/8 | 0 |

At K=2 the witnesses are single-vertex defects, up to translation. At K=3,n=4, the supplied witness has negative vertices encoded by integer 14, root coordinate 0, physical cost numerator 6 and influence numerator 16. That finite 3/8 cost is not a growing-family refutation. K=4 has no bad node through n=4; this is finite evidence only.

code/verify_family.py independently constructs the point-defect truth tables for the frozen domain n=1..10, directly enumerates odd 2-faces and physical boundaries, and rechecks the Fourier coefficients and rational inequalities in the sign proof. The first v1 checker was stopped manually: its large exact powers at n near 10 were unnecessary and threatened the pilot budget. It produced no completed certificate and no claimed assertions. Its cap check was between dimensions and did not interrupt a single expensive power operation; the process was manually interrupted, and actual in-process runtime was not captured. This runtime-control defect was removed with the inexpensive rational certificate. Version 2 replaces those powers by the exact rational A/D>=9 comparison and the analytic log2(9)>2; out/FAMILY_SPEC.json freezes that revision. It passed 2185 assertions in 0.0302 seconds. The v1 memory estimate 5 MB was too low for the unnecessarily large powers; it was not a heavy search, and no dimension escalation was used. No GPU, background job, random search, floating sign decision or cross-agent write was used.

# A025 stage 2: the specified nested rare-flip tower has a paid outer-control face budget

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI remains OPEN. B05 v2 assigns the nested bent/one-point-flip tower at depths 2 and 3, the four frozen orders, and exact bad-node odd-face credit divided by total influence. Bad means the strict inequality B_i>4 Inf_i, with B_i=H(S_i|S_-i) in bits. The physical charge is Q_bad,4(T)=sum_bad pi_v I(g_v h_v)/2. No spectral-conditioning probability is substituted for pi_v.

We define the tower explicitly rather than asserting a theorem about an unspecified mechanism. E0=1; Ej=Aj(Dbent(xj)+1_{xj=0} yj E(j-1)); Fj=1-2Ej. All level blocks are disjoint, each has k data bits, r controls active at zero, and one selector. The preceding tower's negative event triggers the next bent core's one-point flip. Depth one is precisely the B05 activated selector baseline. Dimension is L(k+r+1) in the frozen equal-control cases; the theorem also allows arbitrary different r_j. This construction is one concrete nested rare-flip family, not all possible rare towers.

Result: for all L>=1, all fixed even k>=2 and arbitrary r_j>=0, every complete tree querying the outermost controls first satisfies Q_bad,4/I <=1/2+5/k<=3. This includes the requested control-first and control-data-selector orders. No divergent counterfamily was found or claimed. The selector-first/data-first finite ledgers are exact evidence only; the theorem does not cover every order on every Boolean family.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md contains the complete derivation, face ownership counts and uniform inequalities. Here are the key quantities. With delta=2^-k, beta=2^(-k/2), a_j=2^-r_j, p_j=P(Ej=1), i_j=I(Fj), q_j=Q(Fj), and base (1,0,0), the unactivated level-j core satisfies

    c_j=(1-beta)/2+delta p_prev/2,
    J_j=k/2+delta[(k+1)p_prev+i_prev/2],
    R_j=k/4+delta/2[k(k-1)p_prev+(k+1)i_prev+q_prev].

The activated moments are exactly

    p_j=a_j c_j,
    i_j=a_j[J_j+2r_j c_j],
    q_j=a_j[R_j+r_j J_j+r_j(r_j-1)c_j].

Each unordered face pair is counted once. Data-data faces through the flipped point, data-selector, data-previous, selector-previous and previous-previous faces give R_j. Control-core and control-control pairs give the two remaining activated terms. Thus the proof keeps the cross-level faces that a naive sum of isolated root charges would miss.

For all integer r, r2^-r<=1/2 and r(r-1)2^-r<=3/4; for even k>=2, delta<=1/4, kdelta<=1/2 and k^2delta<=1. Induction gives i_j<=k+2 and q_j<=k+4 uniformly in depth and all r_j. Consequently R_L<=k/4+5/2. The lower bound i_L>=a_L k/2 is preserved.

A split with one constant child has exactly B=2d(1-d)+Psi(d^2,(1-d)^2). Its entropy bound gives B/d<=2+log2(e)/sqrt(e)<3. The outermost controls therefore incur no K=4 bad cost. They leave one active core branch of physical probability a_L. The entire remaining bad-face cost, whatever subsequent complete order is used, is at most a_L R_L by the face telescope. Therefore

    Q_bad,4(T)/I(F_L) <= a_L R_L/(a_L k/2)
                         <=1/2+5/k<=3.

This is one owned post-outer-control face reserve. It does not spend i_L anew at each nested level. For k>=4 the displayed bound is <=7/4. Terminal entropy is zero because the computed/theorem trees are complete with constant leaves. An early stop at the exposed core would retain H(core); it is not paid by declaring the controls finished.

First unsupported universal line: construct a comparable single-owned reserve after exposing overlapping control events while retaining a sufficient physical-influence lower bound, for every Boolean function. In particular the moment induction relies on disjoint blocks and a bent baseline with influence k/2. It does not transport arbitrary terminal core entropy, handle colliding outer events or prove universal FEI. The B05 existential localized bridge is still open; the specified tower no longer supplies an obstruction to it.

# A025 stage 3: point-separator reserve replay

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI remains OPEN. This stage extends A023's one-bit overlapping-event
controller to a point-selector block, separating the complete face price
from the actual bad-node price. Entropy is in bits, outputs are signs,
inputs are independent uniform bits, and histories have physical reach
probability. Bad means strictly B_i>4 Inf_i. J(A)=I(1-2A)/2.

Let S,W,U,V be compatible point indicators on disjoint blocks of widths
s,L,R,T, and let g be an independent balanced Boolean core. Set
A=WUV OR SU OR SV and F=1+A(g-1). All widths are positive integers.
For R=T=1 and every s,L>=1, the policy exposing S first and then the
appropriate WUV or U OR V branch has zero localized bad-node prefix
cost, and keeps terminal entropy pi H(g). This is a quantified class
result, not an all-controller theorem.

The actual Boolean rare-event family F_s has L=2s, R=T=1 and g=z.
For the explicitly defined unfiltered separator reservation
R_all=sum_reached_separator_nodes reach*I(child_product)/2, it satisfies
R_all/I(F_s)>=11s/48 and
R_all/(I(F_s)-I_separator)>=11s(s+1)/60. This refutes every finite constant
in that repeated COMPLETE-price rule. It refutes neither A023's one-bit
v2 theorem nor a bad-node-only rule, nor an existential stopping route,
nor FEI. Its actual complete-tree bad-node bill is zero, and H(F_s)<7I(F_s).

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

- EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, read in full;
  field contract, dispatch, review and frozen-compute sections.

  opening/B05 reread showing the supervisor's 00:50 UTC update.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md is the complete self-contained derivation. Its new point-switch
criterion is valid for arbitrary Boolean branches G,H: if
gamma=P(G!=H)>0 and rho=2^-(j-1), then at a live point-selector query

    B_i/Inf_i <= 2+log2(e)/sqrt(e gamma).

Thus gamma>=1/4 implies every such query is strictly 4-good, even when
the actual coordinate influence tends to zero. This uses paired Fourier
rows, exact residual-selector multiplicities and a log-sum bound; it does
not substitute h2(d) for the actual spectral erasure entropy.

Write alpha=2^-s, eta=2^-(L+R+T), e=2^-R+2^-T-2^-(R+T)-eta,
JB=(L+R+T)eta and JC=R*2^-R*(1-2^-T)+T*2^-T*(1-2^-R).
The directional cuts of WUV and U OR V are disjoint, giving JD=JB+JC.
The exclusive physical accounts are

    pi=eta+alpha e,
    I=s alpha e+(1-alpha)JB+alpha JC+pi I(g),
    R_all=alpha[s JD+e s(s-1)/2+s e I(g)].

The residual-selector term e s(s-1)/2 causes repeated full-price failure.
For the counterfamily, eta=alpha^2/4, e>=11/16, s alpha<=1/2,
I/alpha<=(3/4)s+15/8<=s+2 and
(I-I_separator)/alpha<=15/8. Also
R_all/alpha>=11s(s+1)/32, proving the two stated all-s bounds exactly.
I tends to zero and P(F_s=-1)=pi/2 tends to zero; these are actual sign
functions in dimension 3s+3, with no relaxed spectra.

For R=T=1, the switched branches have gamma=e/2>=5/16. Their selector
nodes are good by the new criterion. The inactive branch is a conjunction
and has constant-child splits with B/d<3. The active OR branch first has
d=1/4 and B<=h2(1/4)<1; its remaining control split has a constant child.
Stopping at g keeps pi H(g) and removes exactly I_separator+I_other.

For completeness, the JS bank used to telescope entropy follows from
the scalar inequality

    [p ln(2p/(p+q))+q ln(2q/(p+q))]/2
      <= (sqrt(p)-sqrt(q))^2/2.

Put x=(p-q)/(p+q). Its proof reduces to
[(1+x)ln(1+x)+(1-x)ln(1-x)]/2<=1-sqrt(1-x^2);
differentiate and use artanh(x)<=x/sqrt(1-x^2), itself obtained by
comparing derivatives for 0<=x<1. Symmetry and limits cover the other
cases. For squared child Fourier coefficients, sum(|a|-|b|)^2 is at
most sum(a-b)^2=4d. Hence JS<=2d/ln2. The good policy gives

    H(F)<=(4+2/ln2)(I_separator+I_other)+pi H(g).

For g=z the terminal entropy is zero, and the terminal literal query has
B=0. This proves the full-tree zero bad-node bill and H(F_s)<7I(F_s).

First unsupported universal line: when the switched branch disagreement
gamma<1/4, no general history-measurable bad-node-only reservation has
been proved that debits each shared control/core account only once and
retains the complete terminal entropy. The sufficient good-node estimate
does not resolve that regime.

# A025 stage 4: nested terminal-coordinate reserve exhaustion

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI is OPEN. All functions are actual uniform-cube Boolean signs;
entropy is in bits and every history has its ordinary physical reach.
This stage consumes A023's whole point-block entropy payment and tests
its explicitly unproved nested ownership step, instead of repeating the
now-paid small-disagreement overlap class.

The exact false extension is: at every reached nested point block mark
its unchanged reserve R_i=2alpha(1-alpha)Inf_i(GH), and pay the cumulative
debit from C times the final physical terminal influence of the SAME data
coordinate i, for one absolute finite C. No transfer from other coordinates
is allowed in that rule. It is an all-coordinate final-terminal-bank rule,
not a universal stopping policy or a claim A023 actually makes.

For all L>=1 and all positive widths s_1,...,s_L, define actual signs
F_(L+1)=z and F_j=1+A_(s_j)(F_(j+1)-1), with fresh disjoint compatible
point blocks and one shared independent literal z. Let delta=2^-sum s_j.
Then the exact renewed z-coordinate reserve satisfies

    D_z=2delta sum_j(1-2^-s_j), T_z=delta,
    D_z/T_z=2sum_j(1-2^-s_j)>=L.

For every finite C choose L>C: the defined coordinate-bank rule fails.
The same function flattened into one point block has reserve
R_flat,z=2delta(1-delta)<2T_z. For one-bit blocks,
D_z/I(F_1)=L/(L+1)<1 and the actual complete-tree Q_bad,4 is zero.
Thus no failure against full influence, the existential localized route,
the source's no-renewal theorem, or FEI is claimed. The point-activation
family itself is familiar; the new target closed here is its exact nested
terminal-coordinate debit and owner-preserving flattening identity.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md gives the all-width/all-depth construction and exact physical
coordinate ledger. A node j is reached with probability product_(k<j)
alpha_k. Its product of source branches is exactly the actual continuation
F_(j+1); its z influence is product_(k>j)alpha_k. Thus its physical debit
is 2delta(1-alpha_j). All inactive terminal z influences are zero, so the
positive debit cannot be assigned to another inactive owner on that same
coordinate. Summation proves the unbounded terminal-coordinate ratio.

The finite witness L=3 with unit widths has D_z=3/8, T_z=1/8. For a
two-block vector the exact excess over flattening is
2delta(1-alpha_1)(1-alpha_2)>0. This turns the finite reserve-overlap
failure into a rigorous parameterized Boolean family, with no extrapolation
from floating-point entropy or a formal non-Boolean spectrum.

The single flattened controller has the same final literal terminal with
probability delta, one reserve below twice its physical influence, and
complete terminal entropy delta H(z)=0. Its constant-child control queries
have B/d<3, and the terminal literal has B=0, proving Q_bad,4=0 and H<6I.
Unused control influence could pay the renewed coordinate debit; this
possibility is explicitly preserved and prevents a stronger refutation.

REVIEW_A023.md independently reconstructs and accepts ONLY the added
whole-block v3 theorem, including actual entropy, minority/KL, terminal
coordinate fractions and its explicit bent/edit/activation consequences.
It reconstructs the paired-row bound, finite geometric cancellation,
disagreement edge isoperimetry, scalar JS proof, all-width constants and
terminal certificates. No substantive repair is supplied. Earlier bundled
v1/v2 portions remain outside this acceptance. This substantive source
review precedes the narrow new counterfamily, respecting the review cap;
no new broad universal candidate is opened.

First unsupported universal line: a three-terminal nested shared-data
controller with distinct nonconstant inactive cores has no proved rule
that merges inherited disagreements and terminal capacities without
renewing consumed fractions. Compatible constant-failure chains flatten;
